We evaluate two computationally efficient temporal decomposition strategies for removing duplicated note text: (1) an embedding-space decomposition using singular value decomposition on local history subspaces, and (2) an interpretable sentence-level diff operation that filters out previously documented sentences before text encoding. Using real-world ICU data, we demonstrate that state representations constructed by stripping temporal note redundancy significantly outperform both structured-only and raw-note baselines across multiple off-policy evaluation methods (Model-Based Rollouts, Fitted Q-Evaluation, Weighted Importance Sampling, and Weighted Doubly Robust Evaluation). Our findings show that explicitly isolating new clinical information from repeated note text yields higher-quality state representations and directly improves RL performance for clinical decision support.

</details>


### [58] [BiasTrace: Linking Reasoning Behaviours to Biased Outputs in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14161)
*Varsha Ramineni,Hossein A. Rahmani,Jerome Ramos,Karin Sevegnani,Emine Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BiasTrace，一种用于标注LLM推理轨迹中偏见相关行为的注释方案，能够将推理行为与有偏输出联系起来。研究发现偏见输出往往源于微妙的推理行为（如无根据的人口统计假设、过度思考）而非显性偏见语言，推理级标注可提升偏见检测效果，且这些行为可用于推理时偏见缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏见测量与缓解工作主要关注模型的最终输出，对产生偏见结果的内部机制理解有限。虽然LLM推理能力的最新进展提供了研究偏见的新视角，但推理与偏见之间的联系仍不清晰，现有方法主要关注最终答案正确性或显性偏见语言，忽略了推理过程中不同行为如何驱动偏见结果。

Method: 引入BiasTrace注释方案，用于标注模型生成推理轨迹中的推理行为并将其与偏见结果关联。该方案捕获偏见特定行为（如无根据的人口统计假设）以及可能隐式促成偏见的通用推理模式（如过度思考）。通过验证过的LLM-as-a-judge方法在偏见敏感场景中规模化应用BiasTrace，生成大规模标注数据集。

Result: 分析表明，有偏输出往往源于微妙的推理行为而非显性偏见语言；推理级标注能够改进偏见检测效果；BiasTrace所识别出的行为可被利用用于推理时的偏见缓解。

Conclusion: 研究强调需要检视更广泛的推理模式以更好地理解LLM中的偏见，推理层面是理解和缓解偏见的关键切入点。

Abstract: LLMs exhibit social biases that can produce inaccurate and discriminatory inferences, posing risks in high-stakes applications. While prior work has made progress in measuring and mitigating bias, it largely focuses on final outputs of models, with limited understanding of the mechanisms that produce biased outcomes. Recent advances in LLM reasoning offers a new lens for investigating bias, yet the link between reasoning and bias remains poorly understood. Existing approaches focus primarily on final answer correctness or explicitly biased language, overlooking different behaviours in reasoning that can drive biased outcomes. We introduce BiasTrace, an annotation scheme for labelling reasoning behaviours in model-generated traces and linking them to biased outcomes. BiasTrace captures bias-specific behaviours (e.g., unsupported demographic assumptions) as well as general reasoning patterns that may implicitly contribute to bias (e.g. overthinking). We apply BiasTrace to reasoning traces in bias-sensitive contexts, scaled using validated LLM-as-a-judge methods, producing a large annotated dataset. Our analysis shows that biased outputs often stem from subtle reasoning behaviours rather than explicitly biased language, and that reasoning-level annotations improve bias detection. We further show that BiasTrace behaviours can be exploited for inference-time mitigation. These findings underscore the importance of examining a broader range of reasoning patterns to better understand bias in LLMs.

</details>


### [59] [Can Language Models Understand mmWave Data? Benchmarking Large Language Models for mmWave Radar-Based Human Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14179)
*Jeongwan Shin,Jaehyeon Kim,Donguk Ko,Jaeho Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出mmWave-QA，首个面向毫米波人类感知的语言条件基准。通过将毫米波点云序列化为简洁自然语言文本，让现有LLM在问答（QA）设置下直接处理雷达感知任务，解决雷达-语言配对稀缺、跨数据集异质性强、缺少基础毫米波编码器三大瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM具有强大的推理和生成能力，但将其与毫米波（mmWave）模态结合的研究几乎空白。尽管视觉语言模型（VLM）已在视觉感知中融入推理能力，但毫米波在低光和遮挡条件下的独特优势尚未被LLM生态充分利用。主要障碍包括：雷达-语言配对数据稀缺、跨数据集异质性严重、以及缺乏基础毫米波编码器。

Method: 提出一种最小文本化接口（minimal textualization interface），将每个毫米波点云序列化为简洁自然语言描述，使现成的LLM能够在QA设置下直接工作。基于此构建mmWave-QA基准：整合异构的公开毫米波数据集，通过校准感知预处理和全局分类法对齐进行统一，并提供自然语言QA标注。基准覆盖6种场景和5类QA任务，支持跨硬件和实验条件的标准化评估。

Result: 在mmWave-QA上对多种LLM进行了评估和分析，验证了LLM在雷达感知中具有零样本推理潜力，并展示了在视觉退化条件下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: mmWave-QA为毫米波-LLM集成研究建立了可扩展的基准基础，为未来在统一框架下探索LLM驱动的雷达感知提供了标准化平台，推动以语言为接口的毫米波感知研究发展。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown remarkable reasoning and generative capabilities, motivating their use as universal reasoning engines for perception. While modern approaches such as vision-language models (VLMs) have attempted to incorporate reasoning capabilities into visual sensing, the integration of LLMs with the millimeter-wave (mmWave) modality-despite its unique advantages under low light and occlusion-remains largely unexplored. The principal bottlenecks stem from the scarcity of radar language pairs, severe cross-dataset heterogeneity, and the absence of a foundational mmWave encoder. We address this gap through a minimal textualization interface that serializes each mmWave point cloud into concise natural language, allowing off-the-shelf LLMs to operate in a question answering (QA) setting. Building on this, we present mmWave-QA, the first benchmark for language-conditioned mmWave human perception. mmWave-QA aggregates heterogeneous public mmWave datasets and harmonizes them via calibration-aware preprocessing and global taxonomy alignment, while providing natural language QA. Spanning six scenarios and five QA tasks, the benchmark enables standardized evaluation across diverse mmWave hardware and experimental conditions, establishing a foundation for scalable research on mmWave-LLM integration. We further evaluate and analyze LLMs on our mmWave-QA, highlighting their zero-shot reasoning potential for radar perception, as well as their robustness under visual degradation.

</details>


### [60] [FreeBalance: Pre-Routing Online Moe Load Balancing via Residual Workload Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14205)
*Pengfei Chen,Yize Wu,Shouxu Kuang,Ke Gao,Ling Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FreeBalance是一个无损的在线负载均衡框架，通过残差网络中的跨层隐藏表示相似性来预测路由分布，从而将专家迁移操作与路由前的计算阶段（如注意力）重叠，消除了传统在线均衡方法中迁移开销对关键路径的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在MoE模型的分布式推理中，专家并行（Expert Parallelism）面临负载不均衡问题，最重的rank会拖慢全局执行，增加推理延迟。虽然离线专家放置可以缓解持久性不均衡，但多任务服务场景中路由动态随层和批次变化，需要在线负载均衡。然而现有在线方法必须等路由决策确定后才能开始专家权重迁移，导致迁移开销落在推理关键路径上。

Method: FreeBalance提出将在线负载均衡与路由前的计算（如注意力）进行重叠。核心创新包括：(1)利用残差网络中隐藏表示的跨层相似性构建轻量级工作负载预测器，在路由决策可用之前提前预测路由分布；(2)基于预测结果提前规划专家迁移（proactive expert migration）；(3)设计成本模型限制交换次数，使同步开销完全隐藏在可用窗口内。

Result: 在多个模型和数据集上的实验表明，FreeBalance将max-to-mean rank负载比降低了32.8%，端到端prefill延迟降低了13.1%。具体地，该方法隐藏了每层平均5.1个专家的均衡开销，这些开销原本占关键路径延迟的约8.5%。

Conclusion: 通过将专家迁移与路由前的计算阶段进行重叠，FreeBalance实现了无损的在线负载均衡，在不牺牲模型精度的前提下显著降低分布式MoE推理延迟，验证了提前预测路由分布以隐藏均衡开销这一思路的有效性。

Abstract: Load imbalance poses a major bottleneck to the efficiency of expert parallelism in distributed inference of Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. The most heavily loaded rank stalls global execution due to skewed routing distributions, directly increasing latency. While offline expert placement can alleviate persistent imbalance, practical multi-task serving workloads exhibit layer- and batch-dependent routing dynamics, making online load balancing indispensable. Existing approaches rely on routing statistics collected after each MoE router, requiring expert weight load or migration to begin only after routing decisions are available, consequently placing migration overhead on the inference critical path. In this work, we observe that online balancing can instead be largely overlapped with computation before target routing (e.g., attention), if routing distributions can be predicted accurately in advance. Therefore, we propose FreeBalance, a lossless online load-balancing framework that overlaps expert migration with preceding computation stages via residual workload prediction. FreeBalance leverages cross-layer similarities in hidden representations within the residual network to build a lightweight workload predictor. This enables proactive expert migration planning before routing decisions are available, creating substantial overlap between weight transfer and computation-heavy pre-routing stages. Furthermore, a cost model constrains the number of swaps to fully hide the synchronization overhead within the available window. Experiments across models and datasets show that FreeBalance reduces the max-to-mean rank load ratio by 32.8% and end-to-end prefill latency by 13.1%. Specifically, our method hides balancing overhead of an average of 5.1 experts per layer, which would otherwise account for about 8.5% of the critical-path latency.

</details>


### [61] [APTER: Adaptive Post-Training with Expert-Grounded Rubrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14212)
*Xukai Wang,Liangqi Li,Zhiyue Xu,Jingang Zhou,Xiaoyu Shi,Jiansheng Cai,Bo Zhang,Zhe Li,Xu-Yao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: APTER是一个将结构化领域知识整合到细粒度评估、优化与诊断中的后训练框架，通过专家构建的标准体系生成查询级评分细则，既用于优化信号又用于标准级能力缺陷诊断，在数学推理和医学问答上相对基线模型分别提升多达15.86和8.04个平均分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型进入专业领域时需满足领域约束、包含关键证据并提供完整推理，而现有后训练方法依赖整体偏好或结果级验证；近期基于评分细则（rubric）的方法通常为每个查询独立生成评分细则，在专业领域中这种无约束的评分细则可能遗漏关键要求并在样本间产生差异，阻碍了对持久能力缺陷的诊断与针对性修复。

Method: APTER包含两个核心部分：(1) 专家约束评分细则构建——从领域专家建立的标准框架出发，每个标准代表一个稳定的专业能力；对每个查询，APTER选择相关标准并将其实例化为与源标准关联的查询级评分细则，无需参考答案即可复用专家标准作为可执行的查询级监督。(2) 自适应后训练——将评分细则判定同时作为优化信号和标准级诊断信号；通过按标准ID聚合低分判定来揭示持久性缺陷，并在强化学习过程中触发有针对性的监督微调更新。

Result: 在数学推理和医学问答两个领域的实验均表现出一致的性能提升。跨越三代模型，APTER相对于对应基础模型在数学和医学平均分上分别最高提升15.86和8.04分。

Conclusion: APTER证明将与专家构建的结构化领域知识相链接的评分细则用于后训练，既能提供细粒度的优化监督，又能实现标准级的能力缺陷诊断与针对性修复，从而在专业复杂推理任务上稳定提升模型表现。

Abstract: As large language models enter professional domains, they must satisfy domain constraints, include critical evidence, and provide complete reasoning rather than merely produce fluent responses. Existing post-training methods often rely on holistic preferences or outcome-level verification, while recent rubric-based methods usually generate rubrics independently for each query. In specialized domains, such unconstrained rubrics may omit critical requirements and vary across samples, hindering the diagnosis and targeted repair of persistent capability deficiencies. We propose APTER (Adaptive Post-Training with Expert-Grounded Rubrics), a framework that integrates structured domain knowledge into fine-grained evaluation, optimization, and diagnosis for specialized complex reasoning. First, expert-grounded rubric construction starts from an expert criteria framework built by domain experts, where each criterion represents a stable professional capability. For each query, APTER selects relevant criteria and instantiates them into query-level rubrics linked to their source criteria, turning reusable expert criteria into executable query-level supervision without reference answers. Second, adaptive post-training uses rubric verdicts as both optimization and criterion-level diagnostic signals. Aggregating low-scoring verdicts by criterion ID reveals persistent deficiencies and triggers targeted supervised fine-tuning updates during reinforcement learning. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and medical question answering show consistent gains across both domains. Across three model generations, APTER improves the mathematics and medical averages over the corresponding base models by up to 15.86 and 8.04 points, respectively. Code and rubric datasets are available at https://github.com/AntDT-APTER/APTER.

</details>


### [62] [A Generalized Parallelogram Rule for Proportional Analogies on Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14220)
*Pierre-Alexandre Murena,Marcelo Hartmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次将比例类比关系推广到黎曼流形领域，通过扩展欧氏空间中用于算术类比的平行四边形规则，在球面、形状空间和概率分布流形等非欧空间上建立了类比的数学形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比例类比已在符号领域和向量空间中得到了充分研究，但在非欧几里得空间中的应用受到限制，本文旨在填补这一空白，将类比关系推广到更广泛的黎曼几何领域。

Method: 引入黎曼域中的比例类比关系，核心方法是扩展欧氏空间中算术类比所使用的平行四边形规则，使其适用于黎曼流形结构。

Result: 在球面、形状空间和概率分布流形等多种流形上展示了所提出的类比方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究将比例类比从欧氏空间成功推广至黎曼域，拓展了类比推理在非欧几何中的应用范围。

Abstract: Analogies are quaternary relations of the form "a is to b as c is to d", usually denoted a : b :: c : d. This notion is formalized in particular with the notion of proportional analogy, which imposes some constraints on the valid analogies. Whereas proportional analogies have been studied mostly in symbolic domains and in vector spaces, their use is limited in non-Euclidean spaces. In this paper, we introduce a proportional analogy relation in Riemannian domains, extending the parallelogram rule used for arithmetic analogies in Euclidean spaces. We illustrate the introduced analogy on various manifolds, such as the sphere, shape spaces and manifolds of probability distributions.

</details>


### [63] [MathForm: Scaling Mathematical Autoformalization with Knowledge Retrieval and Verification-Guided Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14221)
*Lushi Pu,Weiming Zhang,Xinheng Xie,Zixuan Fu,Bingxiang He,Hengyu Zhao,Hongya Lyu,Xin Li,Jie Zhou,Yudong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MathForm框架，通过Mathlib知识检索和验证引导的迭代优化构建形式化训练数据，构造了包含约36.7万条已验证示例的Lean 4数据集FormalVerse，并训练了MathForm-8B模型，在六个基准上以8B参数量超越了多个专门的32B自动形式化模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动形式化方法过度依赖模型的参数化记忆来获取库特定知识，且常见数据构建流程通常仅过滤单次输出，缺乏反馈驱动的修订机制，导致形式化结果难以保证语义一致性。

Method: MathForm框架包含两个关键组件：(1) 检索规划器在生成前从Mathlib中获取相关定义和现有形式化来引导形式化生成器；(2) 使用编译诊断和语义一致性反馈对生成的语句进行迭代修订。基于此框架构建了FormalVerse数据集，并通过监督微调加强化学习训练MathForm-8B。

Result: MathForm-8B在六个基准上平均Pass@8率达到88.06%（语法检查SC）和72.37%（一致性检查CC），超越多个专门的32B自动形式化器。在FATE-H和FATE-X子集上分别达到63%和37%的CC通过率，均超过最强专用基线。

Conclusion: 通过知识检索和验证引导的迭代细化，可以显著提升自动形式化的准确性和语义保真度，且小规模模型也能在形式化任务上超越大规模专用模型，为构建高质量形式化训练数据提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Autoformalization is commonly framed as translating natural-language mathematical statements into machine-verifiable formal languages such as Lean 4. However, faithful formalization requires more than translation. Models must map mathematical concepts to the complex hierarchy of types and definitions in formal libraries such as Mathlib, while ensuring that generated statements preserve the meaning of the source propositions. Existing approaches struggle because they rely heavily on the model's parametric memory for library-specific knowledge, while common data construction pipelines often resort to filtering single-pass outputs and lack mechanisms for feedback-driven revision. To address these challenges, we introduce MathForm, an autoformalization framework for constructing verified training data through Mathlib knowledge retrieval and verification-guided iterative refinement. Before generation, a retrieval planner gathers relevant definitions and existing formalizations from Mathlib to guide the formalization generator. Generated statements are then revised using compiler diagnostics and semantic-consistency feedback. Using this framework, we construct FormalVerse, a Lean 4 dataset containing approximately 367K verified examples across diverse mathematical domains and sources. We then train MathForm-8B through supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning. Across six benchmarks, MathForm-8B achieves average Pass@8 rates of 88.06% under Syntax Check (SC) and 72.37% under Consistency Check (CC), outperforming multiple specialized 32B autoformalizers. On the challenging FATE-H and FATE-X subsets, it attains CC pass rates of 63% and 37%, exceeding the strongest specialized baselines in both cases.

</details>


### [64] [Attributing Preprocessing Invariance in Spectral Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14227)
*Dongjun Wei,Hongyi Wu,Yinuo Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出，频谱基础模型（以拉曼光谱模型为例）声称的"预处理不变性"在很大程度上并非来自模型学习，而是其输入归一化（无学习参数）的效果。当两种预处理仅相差一个正倍数加常数时，归一化会将它们映射为相同向量，编码器根本看不到差异。在六个拉曼数据集上，模型并未显著优于其自身的归一化；训练虽然改进了编码器，但控制实验表明模型只学会忽略能够到达它的变换。作者还跨五种模态考察了多个发布系统，发现大多数归一化已消除此类变换，且多个系统却将此归功于学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视"预处理不变性"的度量方式：以往用"一种预处理下训练、另一种下测试"来验证不变性是学习的证据。本文质疑该解读，认为若归一化本身已将不同预处理映射为相同输入，则不变性不能归因于学习。

Method: 以拉曼光谱基础模型为案例，分析其输入归一化（基于光谱自身统计量）的性质：当一条光谱是另一条的正倍数加常数时二者映射为相同向量。通过六个拉曼数据集比较模型与纯归一化的性能；用控制实验检验编码器是否仅在变换到达它时才学会忽略；用数值测试判定给定归一化消除了哪些变换；并跨五种模态复现多系统的对比。

Result: 在六个拉曼数据集上，模型并未显著优于其归一化本身；模型相比原始光谱有提升，但归一化单独也能做到。训练确实改进了编码器（优于随机初始化），但控制实验显示它仅学会忽略能到达它的变换。多数已发布系统的归一化已消除此类变换，多个系统却将该不变性归功于学习；在两个系统上复现比较同样没有增益。

Conclusion: 频谱基础模型的预处理不变性常被高估——它更多来源于无参数归一化的作用而非编码器的学习。评测不变性时必须与归一化自身作基线对比，否则会误将预处理的效果归因于模型学习。

Abstract: Preprocessing invariance is an appealing goal for spectral foundation models: a frozen model should remain useful when laboratories preprocess spectra differently. It is usually measured by training a classifier under one preprocessing pipeline and testing it under another, with preserved accuracy read as evidence of learning. We revisit that reading, using a Raman foundation model as a case study. Such models normalize their inputs before any learned parameter is applied. If that normalization maps two differently preprocessed spectra to the same vector, the encoder receives identical inputs, so the invariance cannot be attributed to learning. For a normalization that uses each spectrum's own statistics, this happens exactly when one spectrum is a positive multiple of the other plus a constant. Several standard preprocessing operations take that form. The encoder should therefore be measured against the normalization alone, which has no learned parameters. On six Raman evaluation datasets, the model does not measurably outperform its own normalization. It improves on raw spectra, but so does the normalization alone. Training does improve the encoder over random initialization, and a controlled experiment shows that it learns to ignore a transformation only when that transformation reaches it. A numerical test settles which transformations a given normalization removes. Across released systems in five modalities, most normalizations already remove transformations of that form, and several of those systems claim that invariance as learned. Replicating the comparison on two of them shows no gain either.

</details>


### [65] [Polaris : Multi Agentic System for Conversational Enterprise Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14246)
*Varuni H K,Soham Sarkar,Jay Kumar,Goutham Krishnan,Tanvi Johari,Avinash Bharadwaj,Santosh Hegde*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Polaris，一个监督者主导的多智能体框架，通过动态任务协调（DTC）机制实现企业级对话式数据分析，将自然语言查询转化为包含查询、可视化和推理的完整分析工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数组织虽然数据丰富但洞察贫乏，受限于企业级信息查询、解释和解读的复杂性，需要一种能够快速访问、理解并基于数据行动的能力。

Method: Polaris引入动态任务协调（DTC），这是一种基于决策理论的编排层，将智能体-任务分配建模为自适应二部图匹配，实现跨查询、可视化、推理等专业智能体的实时协调、恢复和优化；同时结合reason-first、ReAct风格的智能体，将自然语言查询转化为连贯的分析工作流。

Result: 在结构化企业数据集上的评估显示出较高的语义保真度和答案相关性，证明了多智能体编排在规模化交付可信赖、端到端商业智能方面的潜力。

Conclusion: 多智能体编排能够有效弥合企业数据分析中自然语言查询与实际洞察获取之间的鸿沟，为规模化、可信赖的商业智能提供了可行路径。

Abstract: In today's fast-paced environment, the ability to swiftly access, understand, and act on data is no longer optional; it is essential. Yet most organizations remain data-rich but insight-poor, constrained by the complexity of querying, interpreting, and explaining enterprise-scale information. We present Polaris, a supervisor-led multi-agent framework for conversational enterprise analytics that bridges this gap. Polaris introduces Dynamic Task Coordination (DTC), a decision-theoretic orchestration layer that models agent-task assignment as adaptive bipartite matching, enabling real-time coordination, recovery, and optimization across specialized agents for querying, visualization, and reasoning. By coupling DTC with reason-first, ReAct-style agents, Polaris transforms natural-language queries into coherent analytical workflows that not only retrieve and visualize data but also explain the underlying "why." Evaluation on structured enterprise datasets demonstrates high semantic fidelity and answer relevancy, underscoring the potential of multi-agent orchestration to deliver trustworthy, end-to-end business intelligence at scale.

</details>


### [66] [Grounding Without Corrective Control: Truth-Tracking Profiles for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14252)
*Brett Reynolds*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"可回答性"（answerability）与"矫正控制"（corrective control）的概念框架，用于分析大语言模型表征是否真正追踪事实。文章区分了"继承性可回答性"（derived answerability）与"实时可回答性"（live answerability），前者来自训练中继承的证言、连贯性与先前矫正模式，后者来自当前可检测和修复新差异的活跃通道。语言模型是压力测试案例，文本训练模型在需要独立事实访问的任务上会出现"流畅失败"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明某些大语言模型表征具有内容或指称，但即使基础（grounding）能够确保这些属性，也可能缺乏实时的修正通道（live routes for correction）。本文旨在追问这一差距的后果：当模型输出在任务目标下可能出错时，什么条件使其可被问责（answerable）？什么条件赋予矫正控制？这为评估模型是否真正追踪真理（truth-tracking）提供了概念工具。

Method: 提出三大概念：可回答性（输出差异能够影响目标与任务特定配置产生、接受或撤回的内容）、矫正控制（存在实时且充分独立的路径以检测和修复新差异）、路径画像（route profile，记录哪些路径约束该配置及其关联方式）。随后以语言模型为压力案例进行分析——文本训练模型继承证言、连贯性和先前矫正模式，若目标敏感矫正幸存于训练，则提供"继承性可回答性"；而"实时的可回答性"由当前处理新差异的路径提供。预测"流畅失败"应发生在任务需要独立信息通道访问事实时，并分析自我一致性、检索、工具、代码执行、多模态输入和反馈的各自选择性作用。

Result: 该框架产生可检验的实证负担：预测留出的"路径×任务"组合表现，或在不进行概念重构的情况下改进干预选择。关键在于，表面改进（如流畅性提升）与真理追踪改进（如事实准确性提高）可以分离——即模型看似更好但实际并不更接近事实，反之亦然。

Conclusion: 这一"可回答性—矫正控制—路径画像"分解框架为评估语言模型的事实追踪能力提供了一种超越表面性能的概念工具。其核心洞见是：模型是否在真正追踪真理，取决于是否存在实时的、足够独立的矫正通道来检测和修复新的差异；不同类型的干预手段（自我一致性、检索、工具等）应在何种条件下被选择，取决于"路径×任务"的交互关系。

Abstract: Recent work suggests that some large language model representations have content or reference. Grounding can secure either without supplying live routes for correction. This paper asks what follows from that gap. An output is answerable when discrepancies can affect what a target- and task-specific arrangement produces, accepts, or withdraws. The arrangement has corrective control only when live, sufficiently independent routes can detect and repair fresh discrepancies. A route profile records which routes constrain the arrangement and how they are related. Those profiles support analysis of truth-tracking: patterned support for representational success.
  Language models are the pressure case; text-only arrangements provide a task-relative limiting case. Text-trained models inherit patterns of testimony, coherence, and prior correction. Where target-sensitive correction survives training, these can supply derivative answerability (inherited constraint); live answerability is the relation supplied by a current route for fresh discrepancies. Fluent failures should follow when a task requires independently informative access to the facts. Self-consistency, retrieval, tools, code execution, multimodal input, and feedback should help selectively. Route-by-task interactions test the distinctions. The decomposition's empirical burden is to predict held-out route--task combinations or improve intervention choice without conceptual refitting. Surface improvement and truth-tracking improvement can come apart.

</details>


### [67] [TimeSage-EV: A Live Benchmark for Agentic Time Series Analysis in Evolving Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14270)
*Qingren Yao,Yaxuan Kong,Yuqi Nie,Yichen Li,Stefan Zohren,Anna Vettoruzzo,Qingsong Wen,Ming Jin,Joaquin Vanschoren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了TimeSage-EV，一个在动态演化环境中用于智能体时间序列分析的新型实时基准，包含60个真实机构场景、1,485个问答对，覆盖2023年2月至2026年5月，并引入TimeSage-1.0自演化智能体，揭示了现有前沿LLM智能体在时间有效性、外生上下文使用和适应能力方面的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险的时序分析依赖持续更新的数据发布，新观测值可能改变证据基础并影响结论的有效性。然而现有时间序列QA基准大多基于固定数据快照，无法评估时间有效性和考虑截止时间的证据使用，存在评估盲区。

Method: 构建了一个实时基准TimeSage-EV，追踪6个领域60个真实机构场景，生成1,485个按周期划分的QA对，涵盖月、周、日和不定时发布节奏。每个周期内LLM智能体接收时间序列数据和源报告，以被扣留的目标发布作为真实标签，评估状态识别、数据总结和展望推理三个任务。同时提出TimeSage-1.0，一个配备可复用分析技能库的自演化智能体。

Result: 实验表明，前沿LLM智能体在模型层级间存在显著的性能差距，并在时间有效性、外生上下文使用和适应能力方面反复出现失败模式。TimeSage-1.0作为自演化智能体展现了改进潜力。

Conclusion: TimeSage-EV作为研究资源以月度更新形式发布，包含代码、排行榜和失败模式分析，为动态环境下的智能体时间序列分析评估提供了标准化平台。

Abstract: Time series analysis in high-stakes domains relies on recurring data releases, where new observations can alter the evidence base and the validity of later conclusions. Existing time series QA benchmarks mostly rely on fixed snapshots, leaving temporal validity and cutoff-aware evidence use unevaluated. We introduce TimeSage-EV, a live benchmark for agentic time series analysis in evolving environments. It tracks 60 real institutional scenarios across 6 domains, comprising 1,485 scenario-period QA pairs from Feb 2023 to May 2026 and spanning monthly, weekly, daily, and irregular release cadences. At each period, large language model (LLM) agents receive time series data and source reports, while the withheld target release provides ground truth. TimeSage-EV evaluates state identification, data summarization, and outlook reasoning. Experiments with frontier LLM agents and TimeSage-1.0, a novel self-evolving agent with a reusable analytical skill library, reveal significant performance gaps across model tiers and recurring failures in temporal validity, exogenous context use, and adaptation. We release TimeSage-EV as a research resource with monthly updates, code, a leaderboard, and failure-mode analyses.

</details>


### [68] [Intern-S2-Mobius: Foundation Model with Decoupled Knowledge and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14290)
*Kai Chen,Jifeng Ding,Ning Ding,Jiaye Ge,Lixin Gu,Yicheng Gu,Qipeng Guo,Ermo Hua,Haian Huang,Haozheng Hou,Jie Hou,Xiangyu Hong,Che Jiang,Minxi Jin,Cheng Liang,Dahua Lin,Dawei Liu,Kuikun Liu,Chengqi Lv,Haijun Lv,Han Lv,Ningsheng Ma,Biqing Qi,Jianmin Qian,Shiya Su,Youbang Sun,Huanze Tang,Zhongbo Tian,Hanjing Wang,Rui Wang,Ting Wang,Yi Wang,Baiting Wu,Jun Xu,Bowen Yang,Hui Wang,Weida Wang,Haochen Ye,Jiashuo Yu,Shan Yu,Xiaoyi Yu,Qirui Zeng,Qi Zhang,Ming Zhang,Wenwei Zhang,Bowen Zhou,Xinyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Mobius-v0架构，通过全局共享的Memory（FFN）存储知识向量，多个Reasoners（Self-Attn）迭代进行组合推理。该架构将知识与推理分离，利用隐藏状态作为缓存和载体实现知识的高效压缩与推理加速。7B模型仅用62.6%的数据达到基线性能，35B模型实现近4倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer架构将知识存储和推理混合在同一层中，导致知识压缩效率低、推理计算开销大。作者希望通过将知识存储（FFN）与推理操作（Self-Attn）解耦，实现更高效的知识压缩和推理效率。

Method: Mobius-v0采用知识-推理分离架构：全局共享的Memory模块（FFN）负责存储和检索知识向量，多个Reasoner模块（Self-Attn）通过隐藏状态作为缓存和载体，迭代地向Memory查询所需知识向量，并将知识回传给推理算子。该架构支持从零训练和持续预训练两种范式。

Result: 1) 7B模型从零训练，以基线的62.6%训练数据达到与7B Transformer基线相当的下游任务得分；2) 基于Qwen3.5-35B持续预训练的Intern-S2-Mobius，在下游得分相当的情况下，实现了近4倍的端到端推理加速。

Conclusion: 知识-推理分离的Mobius-v0架构能够显著提升知识压缩能力和推理效率，在保证模型性能的同时大幅降低训练数据需求和推理延迟，为大规模语言模型的高效部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: We introduce Mobius-v0, an architecture that comprises a globally shared Memory (FFN) that stores knowledge vectors and multiple Reasoners (Self-Attn) that iteratively achieve compositional reasoning. Using hidden states as cache and carrier, reasoners repeatedly query memory for required knowledge-vectors, while the knowledge is transmitted back to reasoning operators. Through this knowledge-reasoning-separation architecture, Mobius achieves better knowledge compression and reasoning efficiency. Built upon Mobius-v0 architecture: 1) Our 7B model trained-from-scratch achieves similar downstream score as a 7B Transformer baseline with 62.6% of baseline's training data. 2) Our Intern-S2-Mobius, continually-pretrained from Qwen3.5-35B, achieves similar downstream score while delivering nearly 4x end-to-end inference speedup.

</details>


### [69] [Sensor-Driven Mission Synthesis for UAV/UGV Swarms: A TB-CSPN Coordination Architecture with Hardware-Enforced Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14306)
*Uwe M. Borghoff,Paolo Bottoni,Remo Pareschi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向异构无人机/无人地面车辆（UAV/UGV）集群的协调架构，将不确定多模态传感器证据综合为任务动作，同时在执行边界通过硬件强制安全措施保护安全。该架构结合雷达、射频、声学与视觉观测，采用基于主题通信空间Petri网（TB-CSPN）进行编排，支持在部分且不断演变的信息条件下增量式任务形成，并通过咨询智能体（Consultant Agents）与监督智能体（Supervisor Agents）分离解释、协调与执行，实现可审计决策路径与有界时间证据融合，还以独立的模拟安全包络应对网络战环境中的威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在对抗性环境中，异构UAV/UGV集群需要在不确定、多模态、部分及演变信息下保持协调一致，同时必须保证硬件层面的物理安全，但现有架构难以同时满足可审计的协调与硬件强制执行的安全边界。

Method: 提出一种分层协调架构：1）咨询智能体将雷达、射频、声学、视觉传感器输出转换为带时间限制的语义令牌；2）监督智能体通过授权与策略约束释放任务状态转移；3）采用TB-CSPN（主题通信空间Petri网）编排协调层，以守卫（guards）与同步机制约束非确定性；4）在执行边界增设独立的模拟安全包络，对不安全致动器命令进行钳位或否决，与数字协调层解耦以增强对抗环境韧性。

Result: 通过沿海监视案例研究验证了该架构在运行不确定性下能够实现可靠、受治理且物理安全的集群协调，支持有界时间内融合异构证据，并能应对网络入侵、欺骗、干扰及通信中断等对抗性威胁。

Conclusion: 该架构通过分离解释、协调与执行三个层次，兼顾了决策可审计性、非确定性约束与硬件强制安全性，为解决异构集群在对抗环境中的可靠协调提供了一种可行的工程范式。

Abstract: This paper presents a coordination architecture for heterogeneous UAV/UGV swarms that synthesises mission actions from uncertain, multi-modal sensor evidence while preserving hardware-enforced safety at the actuation boundary. The approach combines radar, RF, acoustic, and visual observations with Topic-Based Communication Space Petri Net (TB-CSPN) orchestration to support incremental mission formation under partial and evolving information. Consultant agents transform sensor outputs into temporally bounded semantic tokens, while supervisor agents provide authorisation and policy-governed release of mission transitions. This separation between interpretation, coordination, and execution yields auditable decision paths, constrains non-determinism within the coordination layer through guards and synchronisation, and enables bounded-time integration of heterogeneous evidence. To improve resilience in contested environments, including cyber compromise, spoofing, jamming, and communication loss, the digital coordination layer is complemented by independent analogue safety envelopes that clamp or veto unsafe actuator commands issued to individual vehicles. A coastal-surveillance case study illustrates how the proposed architecture enables dependable, governed, and physically safe swarm coordination under operational uncertainty.

</details>


### [70] [AnchorBench: A Multi-Pathway Benchmark for the Anchoring Effect in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14320)
*Yiderigun Borjigin,Alexander Hermann,Christian Cyron,Roland Aydin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AnchorBench基准，用于系统评估大语言模型中的锚定效应。通过14个模型（10个开源+4个前沿API模型）和大量受控提示，发现锚定效应强烈依赖锚定途径，合理锚点比无关锚点引发更大偏移，且模型在控制条件下高精度并不保证对锚定效应的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于LLM锚定效应的研究通常只评估狭窄的锚定途径集合，且很少区分无关锚点与合理锚点。作者希望建立一个更全面的基准，显式引入锚点相关性维度，系统评估LLM在不同锚定途径下的脆弱性。

Method: 提出AnchorBench基准，它沿锚定途径（如External、RAG等）和锚点相关性轴两个维度评估LLM的锚定效应。在14个模型（10个开源权重模型和4个前沿API模型）上，使用大量受控提示进行系统评测。

Result: 研究发现：（1）锚定效应强烈依赖锚定途径；（2）通过更强途径引入时，合理锚点通常比无关锚点引发更大的判断偏移；（3）当锚点远离证据支持的答案时，锚点影响力通常减弱，在External和RAG途径上最明显；（4）高任务准确率不保证锚定鲁棒性——即使控制准确率超过95%的前沿API模型，仍易受合理锚点影响。

Conclusion: LLM的锚定效应高度复杂且途径依赖，合理锚点对即使最先进的前沿模型仍构成显著威胁。这提示在评估LLM推理可靠性时，需要考虑锚定效应的多途径性和锚点相关性，而不能仅依赖控制条件的高准确率判断模型鲁棒性。

Abstract: The anchoring effect is a cognitive bias in which an initial reference value shifts a later judgment toward itself. This effect is well established in human judgment and decision-making, and recent work suggests that large language models (LLMs) exhibit similar behavior. However, existing work on anchoring in LLMs typically evaluates only a narrow set of anchor pathways and rarely distinguishes irrelevant from plausible anchors. We introduce AnchorBench, a benchmark for the anchoring effect in LLMs that evaluates multiple anchor pathways under an explicit anchor relevance axis. Across fourteen models, including ten open-weight models and four frontier API models, and a large set of controlled prompts, we find that (1) anchoring is strongly pathway-dependent, (2) plausible anchors usually induce larger shifts than irrelevant ones when introduced through stronger pathways, (3) anchor influence generally weakens as the anchor moves farther from the evidence-supported answer, most clearly on External and RAG, and (4) high task accuracy on the anchor-free control condition (Acc$_{10}$: answers within 10 points of gold) does not guarantee robustness: even frontier API models above 95% control accuracy remain susceptible to plausible anchors.

</details>


### [71] [Program-space Diffusion for Morphology-to-Transcriptomics Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14330)
*Ruyter Swann,Dorent Reuben,Racoceanu Daniel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将形态学到转录组学的预测重新表述为转录程序空间中的条件生成问题，利用cNMF提取低维转录程序，并训练条件扩散模型从组织学图像生成程序激活值，从而利用协调的转录变异并大幅降低条件生成任务的维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学成本高且可扩展性有限，现有从组织学预测空间表达的方法大多在基因水平操作，没有利用已有的转录组建模实践，且依赖异质的基因选择策略，导致方法间比较不公平。

Method: 使用共识非负矩阵分解（cNMF）从训练数据中提取捕获协调表达变异的低维转录程序集合，然后训练条件扩散模型从组织学图像生成程序激活值。

Result: 该公式利用协调的转录变异，显著降低了条件生成任务的维度。

Conclusion: 通过将预测问题从独立基因预测转化为转录程序空间中的条件生成，该方法有望实现更公平的方法比较并提升预测性能。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) enables genome-wide gene expression profiling while preserving tissue architecture, but its cost and limited scalability remain major bottlenecks. This has motivated models that predict spatial expression directly from routine histology. Despite promising results, most existing approaches operate at the gene level without leveraging established transcriptomic modeling practices and rely on heterogeneous gene selection strategies, which complicates fair comparison across methods.
  We propose to reformulate morphology-to-transcriptomics prediction as conditional generation in transcriptional program space, thereby exploiting coordinated transcriptional variation instead of predicting genes independently. Using consensus non-negative matrix factorization (cNMF), we extract a low-dimensional set of transcriptional programs capturing coordinated expression variation in the training data, and train a conditional diffusion model to generate program activations from histology. This formulation exploits coordinated transcriptional variation and substantially lowers the dimensionality of the conditional generative task.

</details>


### [72] [Clearing the Fog: Towards Installing and Refining Proactive Exploration Capabilities in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14339)
*Zhizhao Guan,Chen Huang,Ziming Liu,Hongru Liang,Wenqiang Lei,See-Kiong Ng,Tat-Seng Chua,Anthony G Cohn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体的主动探索能力，识别出阻碍该能力的两个基本瓶颈，并提出了一种名为SAFARI的新方法，通过探索性数据构建和带对比信号引导的强化学习优化来增强主动探索，实验证明其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在环境中进行主动探索（主动获取信息以改善未来决策）的能力存在不足，作者首先识别出阻碍该能力的两个根本瓶颈（即标准示范数据中的后见之明偏差等问题），希望通过专门的方法来增强这一能力。

Method: SAFARI方法包含两个核心组件：(1) 探索性数据构建（Exploratory Data Construction），通过合成具有丰富探索轨迹的数据来缓解标准示范中的后见之明偏差；(2) 带对比信号引导的强化学习优化（RL Optimization with Contrastive Signal Guidance），利用对比轨迹对来区分生产性探索与冗余游荡。

Result: 大量实验证明了SAFARI方法的有效性，并提供了对主动探索特征的深入洞见。

Conclusion: SAFARI能够有效提升LLM智能体的主动探索能力，通过数据构建与对比信号引导的强化学习相结合的方式，区分了有意义的探索行为和无意义的冗余行为，实验验证了其效果并揭示了主动探索的关键特征。

Abstract: We study proactive exploration in LLM agents, i.e., the ability to explore an environment to acquire information that improves future decision-making. In this regard, we first identify two fundamental bottlenecks that hinder this capability and then propose \ours, a novel method designed to instill and refine proactive exploration. Specifically, \ours\ consists of two components: (1) Exploratory Data Construction, which synthesizes exploration-rich trajectories to mitigate the hindsight bias of standard demonstrations; and (2) RL Optimization with Contrastive Signal Guidance, which leverages contrastive trajectory pairs to distinguish productive exploration from redundant wandering. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of \ours\ and provide insights into the characteristics of proactive exploration. Our code is available at: https://github.com/GuanZhizhao/SAFARI.

</details>


### [73] [ScienceFlow: A long-horizon agent for ML research, scientific discovery and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14354)
*Mingming Zhao,Jiqian Dong,Kangping Xu,Zadid Hasan,Chengrui Fan,Shan Jiang,Shuai Mao,Ting Lingya,Linyi Zou,Tailin Zhou,Yun Hin Chan,Wenkai Zhang,Zhanhong Zhou,Guowei Huang,Hongliang Li,Wenjing Cun,Zhitang Chen,Mingxuan Yuan,Yanhui Geng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ScienceFlow是一个端到端的自主科研代理框架，通过将长周期研究任务组织为可执行工作区中的科研分段，引入可恢复的可执行状态管理（ESTRA机制）和证据感知的执行控制器，解决了现有自主科研代理在连续性、死胡同恢复和计算资源分配方面的缺陷。在ML、科学建模和数学优化等多个长周期基准上达到SOTA，在完整MLE-bench上24小时内获得70.22%的Any-Medal分数，较此前最佳结果提升4.92个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主科研代理虽然取得了显著成功，但缺乏连续性维持机制、死胡同恢复能力和基于价值的计算资源分配策略，这从根本上损害了搜索效率、浪费计算资源并降低最终成功概率。作者旨在弥补这一关键空白，使LLM代理能够在更长的时间跨度内维持高效、稳定且目标对齐的科研过程。

Method: ScienceFlow框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 将长周期科研工作组织为基于可执行工作区的科研分段，并将研究进度表示为可恢复的可执行状态；(2) 通过ESTRA（Executable-State Transition through Re-Anchoring）机制控制分段间的转换，选择当前活跃状态或归档状态作为下一锚点，决定是继续还是重定向科研轨迹；(3) 证据感知的执行控制器根据资源可用性、剩余预算和已验证的进展来分配物理作业的计算资源。

Result: 在机器学习、科学建模和数学优化等多个任务上进行评估，在多样化的长周期基准上表现出色。在完整MLE-bench上24小时预算内达到70.22%的Any-Medal分数（SOTA），比此前报告的结果提升4.92个百分点。

Conclusion: ScienceFlow的成效证明了高效的状态管理、自适应探索和目标对齐的执行策略对于将自主科研扩展到短周期交互之外至关重要，为长周期自主科研代理的设计提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Enabling LLM agents to sustain productive, stable, and goal-aligned research over extended horizons is a central challenge for autonomous machine learning and scientific discovery, as progress hinges on continuously managing evolving state, exploration decisions, and computational resources. Pioneering autoresearch agents, despite great success, still lack mechanisms for continuity, recovery from dead ends, and value-driven compute allocation, which inherently undermines overall search efficiency, wastes computational resources, and lowers the chance of ultimate success. To bridge this gap, we introduce ScienceFlow, an end-to-end autoresearch agent framework that organizes long-horizon research work into research segments grounded in executable workspaces. It represents research progress as recoverable executable states, enabling efficient exploration, revision, and execution. Transitions between research segments are governed by Executable-State Transition through Re-Anchoring (ESTRA), which selects either the live state or an archived state as the next anchor and determines whether to continue or redirect the research trajectory. An evidence-aware execution controller allocates resources to physical jobs based on resource availability, remaining budget, and validated progress. We evaluate ScienceFlow on tasks spanning machine learning, scientific modeling, and mathematical optimization. Results on diverse long-horizon benchmarks demonstrate its ability to sustain effective research processes, highlighted by a SOTA 70.22 percent Any-Medal score on the full MLE-bench within a 24-hour budget, outperforming prior reported results by 4.92 percentage points. The efficacy of ScienceFlow further demonstrates that efficient state management, adaptive exploration, and objective-aligned execution are critical for scaling autonomous research beyond short-horizon interactions.

</details>


### [74] [Disentangled Shared Representations Improve Morpho-Transcriptomic Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14355)
*Julian Ostermaier,Swann Ruyter,Reuben Dorent,Daniel Racoceanu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了VAE基和对比学习方法（各自含标准与解耦变体）在空间转录组学（ST）多模态表示学习中的表现，发现对比目标在下游探测任务上更优，而显式解耦共享与模态特异的潜在成分能改善重建和探测指标，但其收益取决于模型家族、任务、方向和解耦强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学能同时刻画基因表达与组织形态，为学习共享形态-转录组结构的多模态表示提供了机会。然而，标准多模态模型通常将不同模态压缩到公共潜空间，而没有显式分离共享与模态特异的变异来源，这可能限制下游任务的有用性。作者希望探究显式解耦共享与私有潜成分是否能够改善配对H&E与ST数据的多模态表示学习。

Method: 在两组癌症队列上、匹配的实验条件下，比较VAE基和对比学习方法（各自包含标准与解耦变体）。表示通过跨模态重建、下游探测和跨模态探测迁移三种评估方式衡量。

Result: 实验发现两个主要趋势：(1) 对比目标在下游探测性能上优于VAE基模型；(2) 解耦变体在所选重建和探测指标上有改进，但增益依赖于模型家族、任务、方向和解耦强度。

Conclusion: 显式分解共享与模态特异信息能够改善空间转录组学的多模态表示学习，并为未来的基础模型提供了有用的评估框架。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) enables the simultaneous profiling of gene expression and tissue morphology, creating an opportunity to learn multimodal representations capturing shared morpho-transcriptomic structure. However, standard multimodal models often compress modalities into a common latent space without explicitly separating shared and modality-specific sources of variation, which may limit downstream utility. We investigate whether explicit disentanglement of shared and private latent components improves multimodal representation learning for paired Hematoxylin \& Eosin (H\&E) and ST data. We compare VAE-based and contrastive approaches, each in standard and disentangled variants, across two cancer cohorts under matched experimental conditions. Representations are evaluated using cross-modal reconstruction, downstream probing and cross-modal probe transfer. The experiments suggest two main trends. First, contrastive objectives yield higher downstream probing performance than VAE-based models. Second, disentangled variants improve the selected reconstruction and probing metrics, although the gains depend on the model family, task, direction, and disentanglement strength. Overall, our results suggest that explicitly factorizing shared and modality-specific information can improve multimodal representation learning for spatial transcriptomics and provides a useful evaluation framework for future foundation models.

</details>


### [75] [Designing Sustainable Federated Learning as a Service using Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14359)
*Keya Patel,Sajib Mistry,Sheik Fattah,Deepak Kanneganti,Aneesh Krishna,Mufti Mahmud,Monowar Bhuyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SFLaaS，一个面向碳约束联邦学习即服务（FLaaS）环境的碳约束神经架构搜索（NAS）框架，通过需求驱动搜索空间、消费者级碳排放可行性评估、可持续消费者调度策略以及进化搜索策略，在硬碳约束下联合优化预测性能、消费者可行性与参与覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FLaaS消费者的可持续性约束给维护碳可行的联邦训练带来重大挑战，这些约束常导致消费者参与不可行，并在硬碳约束下造成联邦训练不稳定。

Method: 1) 需求驱动搜索空间：将消费者可持续性画像转化为可行架构区域；2) 消费者级碳排放可行性评估机制：在动态碳条件下评估候选架构；3) 可持续消费者调度策略：自适应选择可行消费者并分配本地工作负载，保持消费者参与和统计数据覆盖；4) 进化搜索策略：在硬碳约束下联合优化预测性能、消费者可行性和参与覆盖。

Result: 在真实数据集和模拟环境上的实验证明了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: SFLaaS通过将碳约束纳入架构搜索与消费者调度的联合优化，有效解决了FLaaS环境下碳可行性和联邦训练稳定性的问题。

Abstract: The sustainability constraints of FLaaS consumers pose significant challenges to maintaining carbon-feasible federated training in FLaaS environments. These constraints often lead to infeasible consumer participation and unstable federated training under hard carbon constraints. We propose a Sustainable Federated Learning as a Service (SFLaaS), a carbon- constrained Neural Architecture Search (NAS) framework for heteroge- neous sustainable constraints. We introduce a requirement-driven search space that transforms consumer sustainability profiles into a feasible architecture region before federated execution. We develop a consumer-level carbon feasibility estimation mechanism to evaluate candidate architectures under dynamic carbon conditions. We propose a sustainable con- sumer scheduling strategy that adaptively selects feasible consumers and allocates local workloads to preserve consumer participation and statistical data coverage. An evolutionary search strategy jointly optimised for predictive performance, consumer feasibility, and participation coverage under hard carbon constraints. Experiments on real-world datasets and a simulated environment demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [76] [Wrong but Useful: Trajectory Value Beyond Answer Correctness in Multi-Agent Messages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14375)
*Chih-Hsuan Yang,Anjir Ahmed Chowdhury,Cheng-Hau Yang,Weijian Zheng,Fernando Llorente,Xiaolong Ma,Xinyang Li,Eliu A. Huerta,Ian T. Foster,Rajeev Thakur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Diverse Hypothesis Deliberation (DHD)协议，通过缓存多个独立生成的消息并回放相同下游求解器来测量消息的"轨迹价值"（对后续推理的帮助或损害），发现错误但有用的消息在所有基准和模型中普遍存在，答案正确性不能决定消息是否有价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体推理系统依赖一致性、置信度或自动化评分来过滤消息，但这些方法假设"可能正确的消息就值得保留"。事实上，错误答案可能包含有用的分解、约束或科学原理。论文旨在区分"消息正确性"与"消息对后续推理的贡献价值"（即轨迹价值）。

Method: 提出DHD（Diverse Hypothesis Deliberation）受控测量协议：缓存五个独立生成的消息，然后对同一个下游求解器（整合器）分别回放每个消息可见或隐藏的场景，通过对比比较衡量每条消息的轨迹价值，即使其可用是帮助还是损害后续推理。结合多个基准、两个开源模型族（gpt-oss-120b和gemma-4-31B-it），并进行受控重复实验与聚焦干预。

Result: 在五个数学和科学基准及两个模型组合中，错误但有帮助的消息（wrong-helpful）普遍存在。在错误答案消息中改变最终正确性的案例里，每个模型均有超过40%的变化是有帮助的。受控重复实验表明可重复的消息效应的数量不太可能仅由回放变异产生（p=0.0002）。对可重复的"错误但有用"消息的聚焦干预发现：完整消息效果最佳；保留其推理比仅保留其答案保留更多成功；完整消息优势的来源仍是开放问题。在同一问题内，重复的轨迹价值证据也能比单纯答案正确性更好地识别保留或移除的选择。

Conclusion: 答案正确性具有信息量，但不能决定轨迹价值。DHD测量了这一缺失的属性，并产生了可复用的标签，用于学习智能体何时应该听从某个消息。

Abstract: Multi-agent reasoning systems often use agreement, confidence, or automated scores to decide which messages should shape a final answer. Such filtering assumes that a message likely to be correct is also worth keeping. Yet a wrong answer can contain a useful decomposition, constraint, or scientific principle. We test this distinction with Diverse Hypothesis Deliberation (DHD), a controlled measurement protocol that caches five independently generated messages and replays the same downstream solver, called the integrator, with each message available or hidden. The replay comparison measures a message's trajectory value: whether making the message available helps or harms subsequent reasoning. Across five mathematics and science benchmarks and two openly available model families, gpt-oss-120b and gemma-4-31B-it, wrong-helpful messages appear in every benchmark-model combination. Among wrong-answer messages that change final correctness, more than four in ten changes are helpful in each model. Controlled repeats show that the number of repeatable message effects is unlikely to arise from replay variation alone (p=0.0002). A focused intervention on repeatable wrong-helpful messages finds that the complete message works best, while retaining its reasoning preserves more success than retaining only its answer; the source of the complete-message advantage remains open. Within the same problem, repeated trajectory-value evidence also identifies a better keep-or-remove choice than answer correctness alone. Answer correctness is therefore informative but does not determine trajectory value. DHD measures this missing property and produces reusable labels for learning when agents should listen.

</details>


### [77] [AgentRewind: Recoverable Execution for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14380)
*Yu Zhuang,Kefei Chen,Yitong Duan,Shuxin Zheng,Jian Li,Xu-Yao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentRewind是一个运行时恢复框架，通过记录代理上下文和受控环境的对齐检查点，使LLM代理在长时执行过程中能够回退到早期状态并利用之前的经验恢复执行。同时提出了MettleBench基准来评估长周期工程任务中的完成率和部分进展。实验表明AgentRewind在多种模型和策略下优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时执行（long-horizon）任务中，早期阶段发生的错误会通过代理上下文和环境状态逐步传播且难以逆转。现有方法主要依赖计划细化和安全检查来减少错误，但在错误发生后的恢复支持不足。

Method: 提出AgentRewind运行时恢复框架，核心思想是记录代理上下文与受控环境状态的对齐检查点（aligned checkpoints），使代理能回退到较早的状态并携带先前尝试的信息继续执行。同时构建MettleBench基准，用于评估包含一系列相关需求的长周期工程任务中的任务完成率和部分进展。

Result: 在多种任务、多个模型、不同执行策略和代理框架上的实验表明，AgentRewind相比基线方法在任务成功率和平均清单进展（checklist progress）上均有提升。

Conclusion: AgentRewind通过运行时状态回退与恢复机制，有效弥补了现有方法在错误发生后缺乏支持的不足，为长时执行场景下的LLM代理提供了可行的恢复方案。

Abstract: Many real-world tasks require LLM agents to interact with their environments over long execution horizons. Errors that occur early in execution may propagate through both the agent context and environment state, and their effects may be difficult to reverse through subsequent actions. Existing methods mainly seek to reduce such errors through plan refinement and safety checks but provide little support after errors occur. To enable recovery during long-horizon execution, we present AgentRewind, a runtime recovery framework that records aligned checkpoints of the agent context and controlled environment, allowing agents to return to an earlier state and resume execution with information from previous attempts. We also construct MettleBench, a benchmark for evaluating task completion and partial progress on long-horizon engineering assignments containing a series of related requirements. Experiments across tasks, multiple models, execution strategies, and agent harnesses show that AgentRewind improves task success rate and average checklist progress over the compared baselines.

</details>


### [78] [Tripwire: Triggering Aligned Refusal via Statistically Certified Safety Neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14392)
*Wei Zhao,Zhe Li,Peixin Zhang,Jun Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Tripwire，一种训练无关的LLM越狱防御方法。该方法通过逐神经元假设检验（控制错误发现率）并配合"效用特异性"过滤器来识别安全特异性神经元，然后采用触发式钳制（trigger-style clamp）将选定神经元钳制在其有害条件均值激活上，注入内部有害输入信号以触发对齐过程中习得的拒答行为。该方法支持两种可证明等价的部署模式：检测器门控的推理时干预和离线偏置补丁权重编辑。在四个安全对齐的LLM和四种代表性攻击上，平均攻击成功率降至至多2.0%，MT-Bench上的效用下降仅为0.5%-5.3%，是现有防御中最小的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经元/路径级越狱防御方法存在两大问题：一是抑制毒性神经元需要大干预足迹，严重损害模型效用；二是基于外部分类器识别安全神经元的方法会误伤对效用重要的神经元。此外，两者都是"始终开启"的，即使没有攻击也会扰动良性请求。本文旨在解决这些局限，提出一个既不影响效用又仅在攻击时触发的训练无关防御。

Method: Tripwire方法包含两个核心步骤：(1) 通过逐神经元假设检验在错误发现率控制下识别安全特异性神经元，并辅以"效用特异性"过滤器排除对模型效用重要的神经元；(2) 采用触发式钳制机制，将选定神经元钳制在其有害条件均值激活上，从而注入内部有害输入信号，触发模型对齐阶段习得的拒答行为。该方法有两种可证明等价的部署模式：检测器门控的推理时干预和离线偏置补丁权重编辑。

Result: 在四个安全对齐的LLM和四种代表性越狱攻击上，Tripwire将平均攻击成功率降至至多2.0%，同时MT-Bench上的效用下降仅为0.5%-5.3%，在所有防御方法中效用损失最小。

Conclusion: Tripwire通过精确识别安全特异性神经元（而非泛泛抑制毒性神经元）实现精准干预，其触发式钳制机制确保了只有在遇到真正有害输入时才进行干预，从而在保持极低攻击成功率的同时将效用损失降到最低，为训练无关的LLM越狱防御提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Neuron- and path-level interventions offer the finest-grained route to defending large language models (LLMs) against jailbreak attacks, yet existing methods fall short of this promise, i.e., they often compromise model utility significantly. Specifically, one line of work suppresses toxic neurons to erase harmful semantics, but since such semantics are distributed across the network, blocking every pathway forces a large intervention footprint. An alternative line of research focus on identify safety neurons using external classifiers. While promising, the existing approaches suffer from compromising neurons that are important for the model utility as well. Moreover, both approaches remain always on and thus perturb every benign request even when no attack is present. To address these limitations, we present \ours{}, a training-free defense that first identifies safety-specific neurons through per-neuron hypothesis tests under false-discovery-rate control together with a utility-specificity filter. Based on this identification, a trigger-style clamp holds the selected neurons at their harmful-conditional mean activations, injecting an internal harmful-input signal that triggers the refusal behavior learned during alignment. The clamp is then realized by two provably equivalent deployment modes, namely a detector-gated inference-time intervention and an offline bias-patch weight edit. Extensive experiments across four safety-aligned LLMs and four representative attacks demonstrate that \ours{} reduces the average attack success rate to at most 2.0\% while incurring a utility drop of only 0.5\% to 5.3\% on MT-Bench, the smallest among all defenses. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Tripwire-65C4.

</details>


### [79] [LLMs Don't Pay for the Jump](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14397)
*Paras Balani,Subhrakanta Panda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以普朗克1900年提出E=hν解决黑体辐射问题为例，论证大语言模型无法完成溯因推理的根本原因并非缺乏具身模拟，而是缺乏"认识论错误与物理代价之间的耦合"机制——固定权重Transformer推理无论规模多大都不具备这种耦合，实验证据显示即使因果难度急剧上升导致准确率从100%降至17%，输出熵仍几乎不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应当前关于LLM溯因能力的争论：Zahavy[2026]认为LLM无法完成爱因斯坦等价原理所需的溯因跳跃，归因于缺乏具身模拟；而Zheng-Xin[2026]和Farmer[2026]质疑具身性对溯因的必要性。本文引入第三个视角，认为比具身更深入的问题是系统内部是否存在使认识论错误转化为足够成本以强制修正的物理机制。

Method: 以普朗克黑体辐射问题为案例：证明仅靠归纳和演绎都无法产生E=hν的假设，其采纳要求"认识论错误与物理代价"之间的耦合。通过热力学耦合正式化这一区分，并比较固定权重Transformer推理与具备该耦合的系统的差异；辅以实证检验不同因果难度任务下输出熵与准确率的变化。

Result: 理论层面：热力学耦合框架区分了具有物理代价反馈的推理与固定权重推理。实证层面：当任务因果难度上升导致准确率从100%下降到17%时，固定权重Transformer的输出熵几乎不变，表明模型无法感知并响应自身认识论错误的严重性，缺乏强迫修正的机制。该结果在任意模型规模下均成立。

Conclusion: 机器溯因缺失的关键成分比具身模拟更深层：系统必须具有某种物理机制，使认识论错误变得代价高昂，从而强制触发知识修订。这为LLM溯因能力的根本局限提供了更精确的理论解释，并指出了超越模型规模的新研究方向。

Abstract: Zahavy [2026] argues that Large Language Models, despite their capabilities in induction and deduction, cannot perform the abductive "Jump" that produced Einstein's equivalence principle, and attributes this limitation to the absence of embodied simulation. Zheng-Xin [2026] and Farmer [2026] question whether embodiment is necessary for abduction, pointing to alternative routes to General Relativity and forms of abduction that require no sensorimotor grounding. Max Planck resolved the blackbody radiation problem in 1900. Planck's move to E = hν required no embodied simulation. It was motivated by a mathematical consequence of classical theory, an infinite predicted energy for a finite measured quantity, that could not be physically accepted. We show that neither induction nor deduction could have produced the postulate and argue that its adoption required a coupling between epistemic error and physical cost. We formalize this distinction through thermodynamic coupling and show that fixed-weight transformer inference lacks such coupling, regardless of model scale. This is consistent with empirical results showing that output entropy remains nearly unchanged across tasks with sharply increasing causal difficulty, even as accuracy falls from 100% to 17%. We therefore argue that the missing ingredient in machine abduction may lie deeper than embodiment: a system must have some physical mechanism through which epistemic error becomes costly enough to force revision.

</details>


### [80] [The Past and Future of AI Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14407)
*Ross D. King*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了AI科学家的过去与未来——即能够自动化科学研究的机器系统。文章回顾了Adam和Eve两个早期里程碑系统，指出当前的核心问题不再是单个科学组件的自动化，而是系统集成，并展望了AI科学家在加速科学发现方面的潜力及Nobel Turing Challenge的目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基础模型、自主智能体和实验室机器人技术的飞速发展，构建远比Adam和Eve更通用的AI科学家系统已成为可能。作者撰写此综述旨在系统梳理AI科学家的历史演进、当前挑战与未来前景，聚焦于"集成"这一核心问题，并呼应Nobel Turing Challenge这一宏大目标。

Method: 本文采用综述（survey）的方法，通过历史回顾的方式梳理了AI科学家的发展脉络：以Adam作为首个通过假设形成与物理实验循环做出新颖科学发现的机器，以Eve作为现代自驱动实验室架构的奠基者，并从技术演进的视角论证了基础模型、自主智能体和实验室机器人使更通用系统成为可能，进而指出科学自动化已从组件问题转变为集成问题。

Result: 论文指出科学中的各个组件（如假设生成、实验设计、数据分析等）已可实现自动化，真正的瓶颈在于将这些组件与神经学习、逻辑、概率、数学、因果推理、仿真、实验设计、机器人技术和正式科学记录进行有机集成。同时，Nobel Turing Challenge设定的2050年目标进展超前于计划。

Conclusion: AI科学家有潜力使科学变得更快速、更廉价、更系统和更可复现，能够探索人类科学难以处理的复杂系统，并允许多达数千个AI科学家协同攻克单一问题。成功实现这一愿景将创造一种新的科学形式并深刻改变世界。

Abstract: We present a survey of the past and future of AI Scientists: machines capable of automating science. AI Scientists can originate hypotheses, deduce their consequences, design and execute experiments, interpret their results, and revise their beliefs. Such systems are integrated scientific agents, connected to the literature, formal knowledge, mathematical models, simulations, data-analysis systems and physical laboratories.
  Adam was the first machine to make novel scientific discoveries through cycles of hypothesis formation and physical experimentation. Eve established the architecture of the modern self-driving laboratory. Foundation models, autonomous agents and laboratory robotics now make it possible to build systems far more general than either Adam or Eve.
  The central problem is no longer whether individual components of science can be automated. They can. The problem is integration. AI Scientists must combine neural learning with logic, probability, mathematics, causal reasoning, simulation, experimental design, robotics and formal scientific records.
  AI Scientists have the potential to transform science: to make science faster, cheaper, more systematic and more reproducible. AI Scientists could investigate systems too complicated for unaided human science, and enable thousands of AI scientists to work together on single problems.
  The Nobel Turing Challenge sets the goal of developing by 2050 AI systems capable of automating Nobel-quality discoveries. Progress is ahead of schedule. When we succeed it will create a new form of science and transform the world.

</details>


### [81] [Knowing When to Stop: Bayesian Optimal Stopping for LLM Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14425)
*Toby D. Pilditch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 optstop 框架，一种基于精度与自适应停止的 LLM 评估方法，将评估视为顺序测量问题，仅在不确定性高的项目上继续采样，在估计已精确或稳定的项目上停止。在 200 项、10 epoch 的示例评估中，九种验证设置下可移除 57%-97% 的计划试验，且结论与完整运行一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 评估通常使用固定的采样预算，对每个测试项进行相同次数的测试，即使某些项的估计已经足够精确也仍继续采样，造成大量计算资源浪费。作者希望将采样预算按不确定性而非固定重复次数进行分配。

Method: 基于分层贝叶斯推断构建顺序测量框架 (optstop)：在不确定性高的项目上继续采样，在估计精确或稳定时停止；支持二值、有序和连续结果；所有 benchmark 项目均保持可采样资格，无需校准项目库；可在线运行或事后分析；包含安全机制——当测量性能接近零时更谨慎地采样，因为此时稀有成功最为关键。

Result: 在一个 200 项、10 epoch 的示例评估中，optstop 在九个验证设置下移除了 57%-97% 的计划试验，且总体结论与完整运行等效，表明节省幅度取决于评估设计。

Conclusion: LLM 评估的计算量可以依据不确定性来分配，而非固定的重复次数；该方法在保持结论一致性的前提下大幅降低评估成本，且节省幅度受评估设计影响。

Abstract: LLM evaluations often use fixed sampling budgets, testing every item the same number of times even after estimates are precise. We introduce optstop, a precision-based adaptive stopping framework that treats evaluation as a sequential measurement problem: keep sampling where uncertainty remains high, and stop where estimates are precise or stable enough. The framework builds on hierarchical Bayesian inference, supports binary, ordinal, and continuous outcomes, and keeps every benchmark item eligible for sampling, without requiring a calibrated item bank. It runs live or retrospectively, and includes a safeguard that samples more cautiously as measured performance approaches zero, where rare successes matter most. In an illustrative 200-item, 10-epoch evaluation, it removes 57%-97% of planned trials across nine validation settings, with overall conclusions equivalent to the full run. These results show that LLM evaluation compute can be allocated by uncertainty rather than by fixed repetition counts, with the magnitude of savings depending on evaluation design.

</details>


### [82] [The Dynamics of Intelligence Explosions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14426)
*Toby Ord*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨AI辅助AI研发可能引发的智能爆炸的数学基础，指出奇异增长（垂直渐近线）比既有经济学启发模型所暗示的更难实现，并发现了一类被忽视的“快于指数但无垂直渐近线”的增长速率。同时提出生成时间（反馈回路迭代周期）是关键但被忽视的参数，只有生成时间快速趋近零时才能实现奇异增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI被越来越多地用于辅助AI研发，在特定条件下可能形成反馈回路并引发智能爆炸。作者希望从数学上厘清智能爆炸的动力学机制，尤其是识别驱动爆炸的关键因素，避免既有模型过于乐观的预测。

Method: 采用数学建模方法，分析智能爆炸中最具爆发性的可能性，研究不同增长速率下的系统行为，重点考察反馈回路的生成时间参数对增长动力学的影响。

Result: 发现奇异增长（趋向垂直渐近线）比经济学启发的近期建模所预期的更难达成；识别出一类重要但被忽视的增长速率——快于指数增长但不会产生垂直渐近线；揭示生成时间是决定智能爆炸行为的关键被忽视参数，奇异增长的必要条件是生成时间迅速趋近于零。

Conclusion: 智能爆炸的动力学比以往模型所暗示的更为复杂和微妙。真正意义上的“奇点”式增长（垂直渐近线）需要极其严苛的条件（生成时间快速逼近零），这为评估AI自我改进的风险提供更审慎的理论框架。

Abstract: AI is increasingly being used to help with AI R&D. Under certain conditions this feedback loop might be able to produce an intelligence explosion, with rapidly escalating AI capabilities. I explore the mathematics of the most explosive possibilities, with an eye to understanding what drives the dynamics. I show that singular growth (towards a vertical asymptote) is harder to achieve than would be expected from recent economics-inspired modelling, and that there is an important but neglected class of growth rates that are faster than exponential but don't lead to a vertical asymptote. I draw out the generation time (the time to go around the feedback loop) as a neglected parameter that plays a pivotal role in determining the behaviour of any intelligence explosion --- one cannot have singular growth unless the generation time rapidly approaches zero.

</details>


### [83] [PACE-Bench: Benchmarking Physics Adaptation via Code Evolution in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14441)
*Yuhao Zhan,Bingxiang He,Zecong Tang,Chaojun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PACE-Bench 是一个基于物理模拟器的基准测试，包含6个物理域的144个源到目标适应对，用于评估自我进化智能体在环境条件改变后如何通过诊断性沙盒反馈迭代调整代码设计。基准远未饱和：最好的方法（Reflexion + Qwen3-14B）在完整基准上仅成功35.9%，表明模拟器接地反思优于未经验证的自我修订，而记忆锚定和宽树搜索均存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我进化智能体评估通常在固定执行条件下进行优化，无法测试当执行条件改变后智能体能否恢复并进行适应。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过构建一个环境从源到目标发生突变变异的基准，考察智能体是否具备跨环境适应和迭代修复的能力。

Method: 构建PACE-Bench：包含六个物理域的144个源到目标适应对，每对将源环境与一个同目标、同接口的突变目标环境关联。源环境中成功的代码驱动设计在目标环境中会失败，智能体需利用有限尝试预算内的诊断沙盒反馈，通过迭代过程将源代码适应为目标设计。对来自四种范式的十种自我进化方法进行了对比评估。

Result: 基准远未饱和：Reflexion + Qwen3-14B在完整基准上的成功率仅为35.9%，GPT-5.5在完整预算下仅解决Statics子集的66.7%。模拟器接地反思比未经验证的自我修订更可靠；记忆将智能体锚定在早期设计上，宽树搜索在探索中无法收敛。即使揭示确切的物理变化也无法提高性能上限，表明机制重设计（而非参数推断）是核心瓶颈。

Conclusion: PACE-Bench指出当前自我进化智能体在动态环境适应方面的不足，揭示了机制重设计是核心瓶颈，为未来研究提供了明确的方向：需要开发更强大的迭代适应和机制创新能力的智能体，而非仅依赖参数推断或盲目搜索。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents improve future behavior from interaction experience, yet existing evaluations typically optimize under fixed execution conditions and do not test recovery after those conditions change. To address this gap, we introduce PACE-Bench (Physics Adaptation via Code Evolution), a simulator-grounded benchmark of 144 source-to-target adaptation pairs across six physics domains. Each pair links a source environment to a mutated target environment with the same goal and interface. A code-driven design that succeeds in the source fails in the target, where agents must iteratively adapt it into a working target design using diagnostic sandbox feedback within a limited attempt budget. We compare ten self-evolving methods from four paradigms. The benchmark remains far from saturated: Reflexion + Qwen3-14B succeeds on only 35.9\% of full-benchmark pairs, while GPT-5.5 solves 66.7\% of the Statics subset under the full budget. Together, these results show that simulator-grounded reflection is more reliable than unverified self-revision, while memory anchors agents to early designs and broad tree search explores without converging. Even revealing exact physical changes does not raise the performance ceiling, pointing to mechanism redesign rather than parameter inference as the central bottleneck. Data and code are available at https://github.com/thunlp/PACE-Bench.

</details>


### [84] [Wyvern: An Agentic Framework for Generating Grounded Multimodal Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14446)
*Beatrice Alessandra Motetti,Emilien Guandalino,Daniele Jahier Pagliari,Alessio Burrello,Lorenz K. Müller,Konstantin Berestizshevsky,Lukas Cavigelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Wyvern，一个用于自动生成有据可依（grounded）的多模态技术报告的多智能体框架。Wyvern集成图像、表格、文本及引用参考文献，并实现声明自动修订阶段以确保内容有据可查。人类评估表明其图表信息量在87%案例中优于近期基线，报告有用性在63%-100%案例中优于三种替代方法；自动评估显示引用召回率提升2.3倍、精确率提升1.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人工智能驱动的创新时代，知识增长速度不断加快，人类难以跟上。虽然生成模型被广泛用于合成内容，但往往缺乏信息依据（information grounding），导致生成内容不可靠。因此需要一种能够自动生成有据可依、多模态技术报告的框架来解决这个问题。

Method: 提出Wyvern，一个多智能体框架，支持生成集成图像、表格、文本和引用参考文献的多模态统一报告。框架设计了内容有据可依（grounding）的自动声明修订（claims auto-revision）阶段，以确保生成内容中的每条声明都有参考文献支撑。

Result: 人类评估中，Wyvern的图表信息量在87%的案例中被认为优于近期基线；报告有用性在63%至100%的案例中优于三种替代方法。自动评估表明Wyvern在引用召回率上提升高达2.3倍，引用精确率提升1.6倍。

Conclusion: Wyvern通过多智能体协作和声明自动修订机制，在生成多模态技术报告的同时显著提升了内容的有据可依性，在引用质量（召回率和精确率）和报告有用性方面均优于多种基线方法，为自动化知识整理与报告生成提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In the current artificial intelligence-driven innovation era, the pace of knowledge growth is accelerating, and is hard to keep up with. While generative models are increasingly used to synthesize content, they often lack in information grounding. To address these peculiarities of our time, we propose Wyvern, a multi-agent framework for the automated generation of grounded, multimodal technical reports. Wyvern allows for the generation of multimodal outputs, integrating images, tables, and text with supporting references in a unified report. Additionally, a particular focus is placed on the grounding of the content, with the implementation of a claims auto-revision stage. We conduct a human evaluation study to assess the quality of our proposed framework. The results show that the figures' informativeness is perceived as superior to that of a recent baseline in 87% of cases. Furthermore, Wyvern's reports are rated as more useful than those produced by three alternative methods in 63% to 100% of instances. We also carry out automatic evaluations showing that Wyvern gains up to 2.3$\times$ in citation recall and 1.6$\times$ in citation precision with respect to the baselines.

</details>


### [85] [SheetCompass: Hierarchical Relation Graphs for Agentic Spreadsheet Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14452)
*Panjing He,Mingyue Cheng,Yucong Luo,Li Li,Xiaohan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SheetCompass，一个基于图引导和记忆驱动的智能体框架，用于电子表格推理与自动化。该框架显式建模工作表内及跨工作表的语义结构关系，并在记忆中维护任务相关信息，以解决现有方法将多维电子表格扁平化为字符串导致语义丢失的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子表格在实际应用中广泛使用，但大型语言模型在自动化处理电子表格时面临显著挑战。真实工作簿通常包含隐式的跨表关联、细粒度的列依赖以及复杂的空间布局；现有方法通常将多维结构扁平化为顺序字符串，丢失了表内边界和表间语义，使得LLM无法利用人类专家在检查电子表格时自然依赖的全局空间上下文。

Method: SheetCompass是一个图引导（graph-guided）与记忆驱动（memory-driven）的智能体框架。它显式建模工作表内部及工作表之间的结构关系，并将任务相关的信息维护在记忆模块中，从而帮助智能体在复杂工作簿上进行更有效的推理。

Result: 论文尚未在摘要中给出具体的实验结果或评测数据，但提出的SheetCompass框架旨在通过显式的图结构建模和记忆机制提升LLM在复杂电子表格推理任务上的表现。

Conclusion: SheetCompass通过图引导和记忆驱动的设计，使智能体能够利用全局空间上下文进行更有效的电子表格推理与自动化，克服了传统扁平化方法的结构信息丢失问题。

Abstract: Spreadsheets are widely used to organize, analyze, and manipulate semi-structured data, yet automated spreadsheet reasoning remains challenging for large language models (LLMs). Real-world workbooks often contain implicit cross-table associations, fine-grained column dependencies, and complex spatial layouts. Existing methods typically flatten these multidimensional structures into sequential strings, losing important intra-sheet boundaries and inter-sheet semantics. Consequently, LLMs cannot exploit the global spatial context that human experts naturally use when inspecting spreadsheets. We propose SheetCompass, a graph-guided and memory-driven agentic framework for spreadsheet reasoning and automation. SheetCompass explicitly models structural relationships within and across worksheets while maintaining task-relevant information in memory, enabling agents to reason more effectively over complex workbooks.

</details>


### [86] [Shift Aware Transfer Learning with Adaptive Dual-Encoder Fusion for PM Forecasting in Data-Limited Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14456)
*Shahab Band,Hamed Mohammadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个shift-aware双编码器迁移框架，用于PM2.5短期预测。该框架结合源域（美国10个监测站）知识预训练与目标域（台湾77个站点）特化表示学习，在完整微调设置下达到MSE=21.6575、MAE=3.1383、R²=0.8739的最佳性能，并通过SHAP分析揭示预测主要由近期PM2.5观测和气象变量驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当目标域观测数据有限且源域与目标域统计特性存在差异时，仅用本地数据训练模型难以捕捉复杂的时间动态，而直接迁移学习又可能导致负迁移。现有方法在跨域PM2.5预测中面临这一两难困境，亟需一种既能利用源域知识又能保留目标域特异性的框架。

Method: 提出shift-aware双编码器迁移框架：(1) 使用10个美国监测站的小时级观测数据预训练源编码器；(2) 框架中包含源编码器分支和目标域特化分支，两个分支共同工作；(3) 采用时间顺序的train-validation-test协议，使用台湾77个监测站两年的小时级观测数据进行适配和评估；(4) 设置了四种基准方法进行对比，并进行消融分析和SHAP可解释性分析。

Result: 冻结源编码器的双编码器模型在四种基准中表现最佳（MSE=21.8960, MAE=3.1597, R²=0.8725），相对TL-v1和TL-v2分别降低约7.1%和4.1%的MSE。消融实验发现移除台湾特化分支导致性能下降最大。允许源编码器在目标监督下适配时取得最佳整体结果（MSE=21.6575, MAE=3.1383, R²=0.8739）。SHAP分析表明预测主要受近期PM2.5观测和与污染物输送扩散相关的气象变量驱动。

Conclusion: 源域知识在保留目标域特化信息且允许迁移表示在目标监督下适配时最为有效。本研究验证了双编码器结构与shift-aware机制在跨域PM2.5预测中的有效性，为数据稀缺目标域的环境预测提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Short-horizon forecasting of fine particulate matter (PM2.5) remains difficult when observations from the target domain are limited and the statistical properties of the source and target domains differ. In these settings, models trained only on local data may not capture complex temporal dynamics, while direct transfer learning can result in negative transfer. This study develops a shift-aware dual-encoder transfer framework that combines source-domain knowledge with target-specific representation learning. The source encoder was pretrained using hourly observations from 10 U.S. monitoring locations. The framework was then adapted and evaluated using two years of hourly observations from 77 stations in Taiwan under a chronological train-validation-test protocol. Among the four principal baselines, the frozen-source dual-encoder model achieved the best performance, with MSE = 21.8960, MAE = 3.1597, and R^2 = 0.8725. This corresponds to an MSE reduction of approximately 7.1% relative to TL-v1 and 4.1% relative to TL-v2. The ablation analysis showed that removing the Taiwan-specific branch caused the largest decline in performance. Allowing the source encoder to adapt produced the best overall result, with MSE = 21.6575, MAE = 3.1383, and R^2 = 0.8739. SHAP analysis indicated that predictions were driven mainly by recent PM2.5 observations and meteorological variables related to pollutant transport and dispersion. These results suggest that source-domain knowledge is most effective when target-specific information is preserved and the transferred representation is allowed to adapt under target supervision.

</details>


### [87] [Twin: Playing an Unknown Game with a Test-Time Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14490)
*Alexy Skoutnev,Kirill Acharya,Gaston Longhitano,Madeleine Udell,Kevin Ellis,Iddo Drori*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个测试时世界模型推理系统（Twin），让前沿编码智能体通过模拟和交互自主编写可执行的世界模型，在不依赖人工设计的前提下完成连续学习任务（如ARC-AGI-3游戏）。系统通过重放验证和反例修复机制构建世界模型，在183个关卡中清除了179个（97.8%），并在87.2%的关卡中在获得奖励之前就推断出目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法需要为每个任务手工设计世界模型，缺乏通用性和可扩展性。作者希望构建一个能够从模拟和交互中自动学习任务规则和目标的自适应系统，突破人工干预的瓶颈。

Method: 系统由三个核心部分组成：(1) 前沿编码智能体编写可执行的世界模型，其归纳先验足够强大以恢复游戏的真实转移规则和目标；(2) 重放验证机制，要求模型在采取动作前必须复现所有先前观察到的游戏状态转移，任何不一致都会成为反例用于模型修复；(3) 目标推断机制，当模型无法预先推断目标时通过搜索自动发现。

Result: Twin在183个关卡中清除179个（97.8%），其中158个（88.3%）比人类更高效。基础模型直接得分为7.8%，改造流程后提升至61.1%，而Twin将同一基础模型的得分提升至93.3%，清除25个游戏中的23个。

Conclusion: 构建可用的世界模型比预期更简单，而真正困难的问题在于正确推断任务目标。Twin系统证明了基于测试时推理和反例驱动修复的自动化世界建模方法是可行且高效的。

Abstract: We present a Test-time World-model Inference (Twin) system, in which a frontier coding agent writes an executable world model for completing continual learning tasks, such as ARC-AGI-3 games. Traditional approaches hand-engineer such models, one custom design per task. Each game hides its rules and goal, and our system constructs them from simulation and interaction alone. Its inductive prior over grid games is strong enough to recover the true transitions of the game and the goal on nearly all levels. Replay validation happens in a twin world model. The harness enforces that an action is not made until the program reproduces every previous observed game transition. Each mismatch between a world model prediction and the actual action result becomes a counterexample that is used to repair the world model. Twin clears 179 out of 183 levels (97.8%), and does so more efficiently than humans in 158 out of 179 levels (88.3%). The system infers the goal before any reward on 156 of the levels it clears (87.2%), and in the remaining levels automatically discovers the goal by search. The benchmark scores completion and action efficiency, between 0 and 100, against humans playing each game for the first time. Played directly, the base model scores only 7.8%; an off-the-shelf harness increases it to 61.1%, whereas our twin world model increases the same base model to 93.3%, clearing 23 out of 25 games. Building a usable world model is simpler than anticipated, whereas the harder problem is inferring the right goal.

</details>


### [88] [Split the Labor: Separating Evidence Interpretation from Decision Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14509)
*Zhelun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将"解读单个来源"与"综合多个来源"两个操作分离，并引入四字段证据元组（假设、可靠性桶、理由、出处）来固定接口设计。作者揭示了"计数尺度漂移"（count-scale drift）这一失败模式：对未归一化权重求和做阈值实际上等价于后验阈值，但工作点会随来源数量滑移。解决方案是用校准的对数似然比进行池化，这是算术修复而非架构修复，并适用于语言模型之外的广义评分求和系统。最后在纵向语料上两次实例化该原则，在结果明确前后分别于阅读粒度和学习能力粒度上获益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统通常将多个来源的文本直接拼接成单个提示词让语言模型得出结论，但这混淆了两种需求不同的操作：解读单个来源需要大容量与上下文理解，而综合各解读需要固定算术、跨实例可比性以及可以返回空结果的能力。作者希望将二者分离，并明确两者之间的接口设计问题。

Method: 提出四字段证据元组（hypothesis, reliability bucket, rationale, provenance）作为两类操作间的固定接口；指出计数尺度漂移问题并通过数学分析说明阈值化未归一化权重之和等于后验阈值但工作点随来源数滑移；提出用校准的对数似然比池化修复该问题。在纵向语料上两次实例化：一次在结果明确后（阅读粒度），一次在结果明确前（学习能力粒度），后者采用小型序列编码器加携带删失生存损失的树集成。

Result: 在结果明确前的实例中，所提方法达到0.921 AUPRC，而手工基线为0.805。作者还区分了哪些组件可跨领域迁移、哪些必须逐领域重新估计，并提出五个可证伪预测、三个负面结果以及指出现有比较中仍存在的混淆因素。

Conclusion: 将解读与综合分离并固定四字段证据元组可同时确定两个半部分的正确做法；计数尺度漂移是跨多种评分求和系统共有的失败模式，而对数似然比池化是通用的算术修复。该框架有价值且可证伪，不只适用于语言模型，也适用于更广泛的评分求和类系统。

Abstract: Systems that ask a language model to reach a conclusion from many sources usually concatenate them into one prompt. This conflates two operations with different requirements. Interpreting a source rewards capacity and context. Combining interpretations rewards fixed arithmetic, comparability across instances, and the option to return nothing. Once separated, the design problem becomes the interface between them. We propose a four-field evidence tuple (hypothesis, reliability bucket, rationale, provenance) and show that fixing it determines both halves. The separation also reveals a failure mode in how such systems combine, which we call count-scale drift. Thresholding a sum of unnormalized weights is exactly posterior thresholding, but at an operating point that slides with the number of sources consulted. The slide grows with reader reliability. When source reliabilities differ, the vote rule and the posterior order instances differently, and no threshold reconciles them. Pooling calibrated log-likelihood ratios addresses both problems. The fix is arithmetic rather than architectural, and applies to a class of rules beyond language models: score-summing triage engines, diagnostic panels scored by counting positives, and additive multi-signal detectors. We then instantiate the principle twice on one longitudinal corpus, once after outcomes resolve and once before. The same partition helps in both, at different granularities: over reading in the first, over learning capacity in the second. There, a small sequence encoder on an easy auxiliary objective plus a tree ensemble carrying the censored survival loss reaches 0.921 AUPRC against 0.805 for a hand-crafted baseline. We separate what transfers from what must be re-estimated per domain, and state five predictions that would falsify the framework, three negative results, and which comparisons remain confounded.

</details>


### [89] [Participatory Moral AI Is Not Neutral: The Invisible Hand of Developers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14522)
*Taenyun Kim,Edyta Bogucka,Daniele Quercia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过两项实证研究（N=809）在三个部署场景（AI肾脏分配、AI模拟旷工代理、AI生成逝者形象）中系统考察了道德AI偏好诱导流水线的三个关键环节（特征范围界定、选民采样、问题框架），发现每个环节都会显著影响产生的偏好结果，由此认为仅靠投票聚合无法实现公平透明的AI对齐，每一阶段都应被审计和公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统越来越多地参与社会中涉及道德判断的决策，道德偏好诱导被广泛采用（即对参与者进行调查并聚合一票来训练AI策略）。然而，在诱导流水线中存在三个关键但常被视为技术细节而非规范性问题的选择：特征范围界定、选民采样和问题框架。这些选择往往不透明、未记录，可能系统性影响道德偏好结果，因此需要系统检验其影响。

Method: 研究设计了两个阶段的实证研究（总样本N=809），在三个部署情境（AI肾脏分配、AI代理模拟旷工员工、生成式AI刻画逝者）中开展。研究者逐一操纵三个流水线阶段：（1）特征范围界定——考察哪些道德特征在不同情境下进入投票；（2）选民采样——分析不同政治意识形态群体的偏好差异及方向逆转现象；（3）问题框架——测试不同措辞如何影响意识形态差距的宽度以及道德基础与判断之间的关联。

Result: 研究发现：（1）道德相关特征在不同部署情境间存在显著移动，特征方案不能跨领域直接迁移；（2）约三分之一特征上不同政治意识形态的偏好存在差异，部分差异方向相反，选民池的意识形态构成会改变聚合后的偏好画像；（3）诱导问题的措辞可使意识形态差距收窄或扩大最多达一个完整量表点，框架条件还改变了道德基础与参与者判断间的关联模式。

Conclusion: 这些发现表明，仅靠基于投票的聚合无法实现公平或透明的AI对齐。道德AI偏好诱导流水线的每一个阶段——特征范围界定、选民采样和问题框架——都应当被审计并向社会公开披露，否则所谓的道德AI在本质上仍是隐性的规范性选择。

Abstract: As AI systems make more morally loaded decisions across society, one response has been moral preference elicitation. In this approach, researchers poll participants on hypothetical dilemmas and use the aggregated votes to train a policy that an AI model then applies at scale. Before any vote is cast, developers make three key choices in the moral AI elicitation pipeline: feature scoping, voter sampling, and question framing. In other words, they decide which features go to a vote, which voters to include, and how to present the question. These choices are often opaque, undocumented, and treated as technical details rather than normative ones. We examine each of these choices within a common empirical study and show that each can shape the preferences produced by moral AI elicitation. Across two phases (N = 809) in three deployment contexts (i.e., AI kidney allocation, AI agents simulating absent workers, and generative AI depictions of the deceased), we examine the three main stages of the moral AI elicitation pipeline. First, morally relevant features shift across contexts. This suggests that feature schemas should not be assumed to transfer across deployment domains. Second, preferences differ by political ideology for roughly one-third of features, with some differences reversing direction. The ideological composition of the voter pool can therefore affect the resulting aggregated preference profile. Third, the wording of the elicitation question can narrow or widen ideological gaps by up to a full scale point. The framing conditions also change how moral foundations are associated with participants' judgments. Taken together, these findings suggest that voting-based alignment cannot deliver fair or transparent AI by aggregation alone; at minimum, each stage of the moral AI elicitation pipeline should be audited and disclosed.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [What to Preserve, Where to Adapt: A Depth-Wise Analysis of Forgetting in Continual Gynecological Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13660)
*Amal Saqib,Tausifa Jan Saleem,Numan Saeed,Mohammad Yaqub*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究妇科医学图像分割中的持续学习问题，通过块级消融实验和受控适应实验，揭示了编码器-解码器架构中不同网络深度区域对分割性能与灾难性遗忘的影响，发现瓶颈附近的更新遗忘有限，而浅层编码器和深层解码器的可训练化会显著加剧遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床实践中医学图像数据集通常是顺序到达的，而非一次性全部可得，这要求模型能够持续适应不断演化的数据分布。在妇科图像分割中，跨成像模态、解剖结构和标注协议的巨大异质性构成了极具挑战性的持续学习场景，现有方法在如此大的分布偏移下难以保留已学知识，导致灾难性遗忘。作者旨在深入理解这一设置下遗忘的机制，特别是编码器-解码器架构中不同区域如何影响分割性能与遗忘。

Method: 采用两种互补的分析方法：(1) 块级消融分析（block-wise ablation），逐块消融编码器-解码器网络的不同区域以评估其对分割性能的贡献；(2) 受控适应实验（controlled adaptation experiments），限制参数更新仅发生在特定网络区域（如瓶颈附近），逐步扩展可训练区域（浅层编码器和深层解码器），观察遗忘随更新位置变化的情况。

Result: (1) 消融早期编码器和晚期解码器区域导致最大的性能退化，说明分割性能在网络层级中分布不均；(2) 当更新限制在瓶颈附近区域时遗忘有限，但一旦浅层编码器和解码器变得可训练，即使仅更新少量参数，遗忘也会急剧增加。这表明编码器-解码器架构中遗忘的发生强烈受网络深度上更新位置的影响。

Conclusion: 编码器-解码器架构中，持续学习时的遗忘程度与参数更新在网络不同深度层级上的位置密切相关。瓶颈附近区域的更新对先前知识的干扰较小，而浅层编码器和深层解码器的更新即使参数数量很少也容易引发严重遗忘。这一发现为设计更具针对性的持续学习策略提供了重要指导，例如优先冻结容易引发遗忘的网络区域。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation models are typically trained under the assumption that all data are available simultaneously. However, in clinical practice, datasets often arrive sequentially, requiring models to adapt continuously to evolving data distributions. We study this problem in gynecological image segmentation, where substantial heterogeneity across imaging modalities, anatomical structures, and annotation protocols creates a particularly challenging continual learning setting. Under these large distribution shifts, existing continual learning methods struggle to preserve previously learned knowledge, leading to catastrophic forgetting. To better understand forgetting in this setting, we investigate how different encoder--decoder regions influence segmentation performance and forgetting during continual gynecological segmentation. Through block-wise ablation analysis, we observe that ablating early encoder and late decoder regions results in the largest performance degradation, indicating that segmentation performance depends unevenly across the network hierarchy. Using controlled adaptation experiments, we further show that forgetting remains limited when updates are restricted to bottleneck-adjacent regions, but increases sharply once shallower encoders and decoders become trainable, even when only a small subset of parameters is updated. These findings suggest that forgetting in the encoder-decoder architecture is strongly influenced by where updates occur across network depth during continual learning. Full code and analysis pipelines will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [91] [Multiphase-Diff: Diffusion-Based Generative Modeling for High-Contrast Multiphase Physical Systems with Sharp Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13669)
*Yining Huang,Zhenyu Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Multiphase-Diff，一个用于高对比度、尖锐界面多相场生成的物理约束扩散模型，通过保守通量残差、解析双射表示和 Jacobi 预条件似然三大贡献，解决了扩散模型在多相物理场中的奇异梯度、相位擦除和全局似然尺度失衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高对比度、尖锐界面的多相场在物理约束扩散生成中面临三个相互耦合的困难：(1) 系数跳变处扩展的点态强形式 PDE 残差包含奇异梯度项，会惩罚物理界面；(2) 极端对比度下低幅值相可能低于扩散噪声下限而被擦除、错误缩放或生成负系数；(3) 全局似然尺度使高幅值相主导监督。这些困难阻碍了扩散模型在科学样本生成中的应用。

Method: Multiphase-Diff 提出三个对应贡献：(i) 保守通量残差——避免对不连续系数求导并强制离散守恒；(ii) 解析双射表示——将低幅值信号映射到 O(1) 潜变量尺度，并通过指数解码保证系数正性；(iii) Jacobi 预条件似然——归一化局部残差尺度，实现平衡监督。

Result: 在三个互补的多相基准上，Multiphase-Diff 在物理保真度和分布保真度两方面均优于七个基线方法，并在不同相位对比度和组成下展现出鲁棒性，证明了其在该挑战性场景中科学样本生成的有效性。

Conclusion: Multiphase-Diff 通过通量守恒、解析表示和预条件化似然三个层面的协同设计，有效解决了高对比度多相场生成中的核心难题，为物理约束扩散模型在该挑战性科学场景中的部署提供了有效方案。
