Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world degraded images show that MagnifiQ outperforms prior diffusion-based restoration methods in perceptual quality and human preference, producing sharper textures and more coherent 4K results while offering practical speed--quality trade-offs through its scalable backbone and progressive design.

</details>


### [156] [CPI-Bench: A Comprehensive,Practical and Intelligent Benchmark for Real-World Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14546)
*Qinye Zhou,Jun Zheng,Yongchao Du,Yuan Wang,Zhengrui Chen,Zuan Gao,Taihang Hu,Chao Lin,Yefeng Shen,Xingjian Wang,Zhao Wang,Zhengtao Wu,Xiaoli Xu,Zhengze Xu,Hao Yan,Denghui Yang,Yuhang Yu,Huayu Zhang,Mingzhou Zhang,Mengting Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CPI-Bench，一个面向真实世界图像编辑场景的综合性、实用性与智能性基准测试框架，包含三个子集：CPI-General-Bench（覆盖多图像编辑任务）、CPI-Practical-Bench（面向高频真实用户应用场景）和CPI-Intelligent-Bench（评估高级推理编辑能力）。实验表明其能够有效增强模型间区分度，并与Arena Image Edit Leaderboard达到最高对齐度，可作为人类感知判断的可靠代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑基准仅局限于简单的单图像任务，覆盖面有限，无法有效区分不同模型的性能，且难以评估模型在复杂多图像编辑、高度推理指令和实际部署场景中的表现，因此急需一个更全面可靠的评估基准。

Method: 构建三维度基准框架：CPI-General-Bench首次纳入多图像编辑评估以全面覆盖多样化编辑任务；CPI-Practical-Bench聚焦高频真实用户应用场景；CPI-Intelligent-Bench针对高难度推理型编辑能力进行专门评估。

Result: 基于CPI-Bench对主流图像编辑模型的评估显示，该基准增强了模型间的性能区分度，能够全面可靠地量化模型在通用编辑能力、实际部署效能和高级推理编辑方面的差距，且排名分析与Arena Image Edit Leaderboard具有最高对齐度。

Conclusion: CPI-Bench提供了更全面的图像编辑模型评估方案，能忠实捕获人类评估者的偏好和感知判断，可作为真实世界用户体验的可靠代理，为图像编辑模型的未来优化提供重要指导。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of image editing models and their widespread application across various domains, there is an increasingly urgent need to deploy these model capabilities directly into real-world scenarios. However, existing benchmarks remain confined to simple single-image tasks, suffering from limited coverage dimensions and an inability to effectively differentiate performance among diverse models. Consequently, they fail to reliably evaluate model performance in complex multi-image editing, highly demanding reasoning instructions, and practical deployment settings. To address these limitations, we propose CPI-Bench, a Comprehensive, Practical andIntelligent benchmark for real-world image editing. CPI-Bench comprises three core subsets: CPI-General-Bench, which comprehensively covers diverse editing tasks and pioneers the inclusion of multi-image editing evaluation; CPI-Practical-Bench, which focuses on high-frequency real-user application scenarios; and CPI-Intelligent-Bench, which is dedicated to evaluating capabilities in highly demanding reasoning-based editing. Evaluation results of mainstream image editing models based on CPI-Bench demonstrate that CPI-Bench enhances performance differentiation among models. It provides a comprehensive and reliable quantification of gaps in general editing capabilities, practical deployment efficacy, and advanced reasoning-based editing, offering invaluable guidance for the future optimization of image editing models. Crucially, our ranking analysis reveals that CPI-Bench achieves the highest alignment with the Arena Image Edit Leaderboard, indicating it faithfully captures the preferences and perceptual judgments of human evaluators, serving as a robust proxy for real-world user experience.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] ["I Thought You Were The Uncensored Place": Norms, Rules, and Moderation in AI-Generated Sexual Content Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13659)
*Lucy Qin,Jaron Mink,Elissa M. Redmiles*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究通过对24名大型AI生成性内容（AIG-SC）在线社区成员和版主的深度访谈，探讨了社区治理如何平衡防止滥用与支持自由表达之间的矛盾，揭示了非审查和非评判规范的复杂性，以及技术、社区和法律治理在减轻滥用内容生产方面的潜在作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI生成性内容（AIG-SC）的激增，出现了大量支持创作者需求的在线社区。然而，这些社区面临一个关键挑战：如何在防止滥用行为（如AI生成的儿童性虐待材料）的同时，支持自由表达。现有研究缺乏对这类社区治理机制及其实际运作的深入理解。

Method: 对24名来自大型AIG-SC在线社区（成员数超过10,000人）的成员和版主进行深度半结构化访谈，这些社区均制定了禁止创建和分享滥用内容的明确规定。访谈围绕社区形成原因、隐含规范、显性规则及其内容审核实践、社区价值观与审核之间的张力等四个维度展开分析。

Result: 研究发现：尽管许多创作者和社区对滥用行为有个人界限，但创建任何形式AI生成性内容的建议和资源对所有用户开放，无论其意图如何；社区中普遍存在反审查和非评判的规范，这使得版主只能以法律和平台服务条款的边界来正当化其审核行动，为滥用行为留下了空间。

Conclusion: 作者认为需要从技术、社区和法律三个层面综合施策，才是有效减少滥用性内容生产的关键路径，并反思了这三种治理方式各自的有效性及协同作用。

Abstract: As AI-generated sexual content (AIG-SC) is increasingly produced, online communities have emerged to support creators' needs. To understand whether and how community governance attempts work to prevent abuse while supporting free expression, we interviewed 24 members and moderators of large AIG-SC online communities (10,000+ members) with stated rules against creating and sharing abusive content (e.g., AI-generated CSAM). Through in-depth interviews, we offer insight into: (1) how and why these communities form; (2) implicit community norms; (3) explicitly stated rules---and their operationalization via content moderation; and (4) tensions between community values and moderation that leave space for abusive behavior.
  Our findings reveal a complex picture: while many creators and communities have personal boundaries against abuse, advice and resources for creating any form of AI-generated sexual content are accessible to users regardless of their intentions. Further complicating community moderation are norms that center anti-censorship and non-judgment, which leave moderators to justify their actions using the limits of the law and terms of service. We end by reflecting on the ways in which technical, community, and legal governance may most effectively mitigate the production of abusive content.

</details>


### [158] [Weird Machines in Transport Layer Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13685)
*Michael Collins,Jada Cumberland,Brianne Dunn,Ross Gore,Samuel Jackson,Sachin Shetty,Jonathan Takeshita*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次将"怪癖机器"（weird machines）理论扩展到TLS握手协议领域，证明OpenSSL和BoringSSL中的合法TLS原语（会话缓存、重协商逻辑、扩展解析、证书验证）可以组合成图灵完备的计算系统，并提出"信任驱动"（trust actuation）概念形式化这种计算与认证/信任决策的耦合。作者通过两个基于真实OpenSSL代码路径的演示验证了该理论：一个用于异常握手检测的防御型sentinels系统，和一个通过中连接重协商绕过密码强度策略检查的认证绕过攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作已在软件系统（x86指令、ELF元数据、页表）和网络物理系统（工业控制网络）中研究了weird machines现象，但TLS握手协议作为一个广泛部署且安全关键的领域尚未被系统性地纳入该理论框架。作者希望将weird machine理论扩展到TLS，揭示合法TLS原语组合后可能产生的潜在计算能力及其安全影响。

Method: 论文首先形式化定义了TLS握手中可组合为计算系统的原语条件（会话存储、序列计数器算术、握手状态的条件分支、基于恢复/重试循环的迭代），提出"信任驱动"概念以描述这种计算与认证决策的耦合关系。随后在真实OpenSSL代码路径上构建了两个工作演示系统：一个防御性的sentinels系统和一个攻击性的认证绕过系统，均在Docker中针对真实服务器和客户端二进制运行验证。

Result: 作者证明了TLS握手协议及其两个主导实现（OpenSSL和BoringSSL）满足构造图灵完备系统的条件，并通过两个真实的Docker演示验证了理论。其中认证绕过演示通过中连接重协商（mid-connection renegotiation）组合标准原语，在不使用任何内存损坏或外部恶意软件的情况下击败了密码强度策略检查。sentinels演示则展示了同一类原语组合成防御性异常检测机制。

Conclusion: 本文拓展了weird machine理论的适用范围，从软件系统和物理系统延伸到TLS握手这一安全关键协议领域。核心贡献在于揭示TLS的"信任驱动"计算模式——计算能力与认证和信任决策耦合而非物理致动——并实证证明其可实现任意计算（图灵完备）。这对TLS安全分析具有重要启示：即使没有内存漏洞，合法协议的组合语义也可能产生超出预期的攻击面。

Abstract: Weird machines are latent computational capabilities that emerge from the composition of architectural components. Prior work has studied this phenomenon extensively in software systems, including x86 instructions, ELF metadata, and page tables, and more recently in cyber-physical systems such as industrial control networks. This paper extends weird machine theory to a new domain: the Transport Layer Security (TLS) handshake and its two dominant implementations, OpenSSL and BoringSSL.
  We show that legitimate TLS primitives, including session cache entries, renegotiation logic, extension parsing, and certificate verification steps, compose into Turing-complete systems whose computation is coupled to authentication and trust decisions rather than physical actuation. We formalize this coupling, which we call trust actuation, and argue that any TLS implementation providing session storage, arithmetic on sequence counters, conditional branching on handshake state, and iteration through resumption or retry loops satisfies the conditions for arbitrary computation.
  We validate this theory with two working demonstrations built on real OpenSSL code paths. The first, a sentinel system, composes standard TLS primitives into a defensive mechanism that detects anomalous handshake behavior. The second, an authentication bypass, composes the same class of primitives into an attack that defeats a cipher-strength policy check through mid-connection renegotiation, without any memory corruption or external malware. Both demonstrations run against real server and client binaries in Docker.

</details>


### [159] [A Reproducibility Protocol for Cross-Implementation Evaluation of Post-Quantum ACVP Test Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13784)
*Christopher M. Frost*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文定义了一个产品中立的可复现性协议（Protocol v2），用于对NIST ML-KEM的三个公开实现（@noble/post-quantum 0.7.0、liboqs 0.16.0、Go 1.26.4）进行标准验证测试。在2,160条基础记录中，所有1,650条可执行评估均与NIST预言机匹配，510条不支持记录匹配Go的预声明能力边界；成对执行重叠部分完全一致，三组冻结对照均产生精确的预声明结果，无任何失败、适配器错误或状态不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码学标准的独立实现应复现相同的已知答案结果，但只有当语料库、修订版本、公共接口、排除项和证据被精确陈述时，一致性才具有意义。作者旨在通过定义严格的产品中立复现协议来弥合这一可复现性鸿沟。

Method: 提出Protocol v2：采用固定的公共ACVP语料库，冻结特定于提供者的能力声明，对称应用统一的验证错误分类法，保留所有选定用例，并区分字节、验证判决、不支持的操作与适配器错误。通过源码构建三个实现，进行三次重复实验，执行2,160条笛卡尔积基础记录，包括1,650条可执行评估和510条不支持记录，另加150条keyGen-ek投影诊断和三组冻结对照（字节比较、判决比较、畸形响应错误分离）。

Result: 所有1,650条可执行评估与NIST预言机匹配；510条不支持记录匹配Go的预声明能力边界；成对重叠完全一致：noble-liboqs 720/720，Go配对各210/210；150条Go封装密钥投影全部匹配；三组冻结对照均产生精确预声明结果；无基础失败、适配器错误或状态不稳定。

Conclusion: 证据建立了有界的作者运行可重复性，并揭示了实际的标准差距：公开ML-KEM包提供了实质不同的确定性和验证测试表面。但独立的第三方外部复现尚未实现观察；结果不构成认证、穷尽正确性、侧信道抗性、安全集成或生产保证。

Abstract: Independent implementations of a cryptographic standard should reproduce the same known-answer results, yet agreement is meaningful only when the corpus, revisions, public interfaces, exclusions, and evidence are precisely stated. This study defines a product-neutral reproducibility protocol for three public implementations of NIST ML-KEM against a pinned public Automated Cryptographic Validation Protocol corpus. Protocol v2 freezes provider-specific capabilities, applies one validation-error taxonomy symmetrically, preserves every selected case, and separates bytes, validation verdicts, unsupported operations, and adapter errors. The source-built experiment evaluated @noble/post-quantum 0.7.0, liboqs 0.16.0, and Go 1.26.4. Across three repetitions, the required Cartesian product comprised 2,160 base records: all 1,650 declared executable evaluations matched the NIST oracle, and all 510 unsupported records matched Go's predeclared capability boundary. Pairwise agreement was complete on every executable overlap: 720 of 720 noble-liboqs records and 210 of 210 records for each Go pairing. A separate keyGen-ek-projection diagnostic matched all 150 Go encapsulation-key projections without counting them as full key generation. Three frozen controls independently exercised byte comparison, verdict comparison, and malformed-response error separation; each produced its exact predeclared outcome. No base failure, adapter error, or status instability occurred. The evidence establishes bounded author-run repeatability and exposes a practical standards gap: public ML-KEM packages provide materially different deterministic and validation-test surfaces. Independent external reproduction remains unobserved. The results do not establish certification, exhaustive correctness, side-channel resistance, secure integration, or production assurance.

</details>


### [160] [The ack3 H1 2026 DeFi Incident Dataset: Audit Scope Across 135 Security Incidents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13792)
*Josef Gattermayer,Jan Kalivoda,Arman Bašović*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 基于ack3发布的H1 2026 DeFi事件数据集（135起安全事件，约9.3986亿美元损失），分析审计历史与事件路径范围的关系。在可识别审计历史的68起事件中，46起（67.6%）的攻击路径超出所有公开预审计范围，20起在范围内，2起未决；按损失计算，范围外路径占94.4%，剔除两起大型事件后仍占72.1%。研究揭示项目级审计历史与事件路径范围是两个独立变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约审计通常仅针对特定时间点的明确限定工件，但"已审计"标签常被误视为项目级整体保障。作者希望厘清审计范围与实际安全事件路径之间的真实对应关系，纠正业界对审计覆盖范围的普遍误解。

Method: 使用ack3发布、覆盖2026年1月1日至6月29日的H1 2026 DeFi事件数据集中135起安全事件，从中识别68起具有审计历史的事件；按事件路径是否在公开预审计范围内进行分类（范围内/范围外/未决），并结合数量与损失金额进行加权统计，同时排除两起大型事件做敏感性分析，还考察了审计年龄、时间损失分布及受影响项目类型。

Result: 68起可识别审计历史的事件中，46起攻击路径在审计范围外（占67.6%），20起在范围内（29.4%），2起未决。按损失加权，范围外路径占94.4%；剔除两起大型事件后占比降至72.1%，结果方向不变。总体报告损失为9.3986亿美元。

Conclusion: 审计历史与攻击路径是否在审计范围内是两个明显不同的变量——项目曾经历审计并不意味着该审计覆盖了实际受攻击的代码路径。这提示安全审计的覆盖范围需被更严谨地界定与沟通，审计标签不应被当作全项目保障来使用。

Abstract: Smart-contract audits cover defined artifacts at a specific time, but the label audited is often treated as project-wide assurance. We analyze audit history and incident-path scope across 135 DeFi security incidents using the H1 2026 DeFi Incident Dataset published by cybersecurity company ack3 (https://ack3.ai), covering 1 January to 29 June 2026. The corpus reports USD 939.86 million in attributed loss. Audit history was identified for 68 incidents. Of these, 46 attack paths were outside all identified public pre-incident audit scopes, 20 were inside at least one scope, and 2 were unresolved. Within this 68-incident subset, outside-scope paths represented 67.6% by count and 94.4% of reported loss. The loss-weighted result was concentrated in two large incidents; excluding both reduced the share to 72.1%, while preserving the direction of the result. We also describe audit age, temporal loss distribution, and affected project types. The results show that project-level audit history and incident-path scope are distinct variables.

</details>


### [161] [Mobile Apps vs. Web Browsers: A User Perception Study with Android Apps and Google Chrome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13803)
*Harel Berger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究通过实验和问卷调查，考察了Android智能手机用户在移动应用与网页浏览器之间访问在线服务时的感知差异，重点关注安全、隐私和可用性三个方面，旨在为开发者改进两个平台提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户在移动应用和网页浏览器之间做出选择时，其安全、隐私和可用性方面的认知与偏好尚未被充分理解，而这一认知差异对平台设计与开发具有重要影响。

Method: 结合实验法与问卷调查法，以Android智能手机用户为研究对象，收集用户对移动应用与网页浏览器在安全、隐私和可用性方面的感知数据。

Result: 研究识别出了影响用户在移动应用与网页浏览器之间选择的关键关注点与偏好，揭示了用户对两个平台存在的某些误解。

Conclusion: 研究结果为开发者改进移动应用和网页浏览器的安全、隐私与可用性设计提供了有价值的洞见，帮助其更有针对性地解决用户的顾虑与误解。

Abstract: This study examines user perceptions of mobile applications (apps) versus web browsers for accessing online services, with an emphasis on security, privacy, and usability aspects. Through a combination of an experiment and a survey with Android smartphone users, the research seeks to identify the key concerns and preferences that influence their choice between mobile apps and web browsers. The findings will offer valuable insights for developers to improve the security, privacy, and usability of both platforms by addressing user concerns and misconceptions.

</details>


### [162] [Vaulted Passkeys: A Device-Bound Proposal for Authenticated Credential Export and Import](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13806)
*Pol Henarejos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Vaulted Passkeys架构，通过受保护的状态导出与恢复机制，解决硬件认证器（如passkey）的私钥可移植性难题，在不暴露明文私钥的前提下实现凭证备份与灾难恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件认证器故意抵抗私钥提取以保证安全，但更换设备、灾难恢复和受控迁移需要凭证可移植性。现有方案通过预先注册额外认证器来降低风险，但这只是冗余而非原始凭证的备份，需要提前准备替换硬件。本文旨在填补这一恢复缺口。

Method: 提出Vaulted Passkeys架构：使用256位随机Kvault密钥保护PKV1凭证信封，通过HKDF分离密钥和四种明确的AEAD（认证加密）配置文件实现保护。设计将注册与导出/导入流程分离，并区分必需的金库组件与可选的身份组件。贡献包括角色分离的系统模型、线格式、威胁分析、实现映射和可证伪的评估计划。

Result: 原型实现证明了该方案的可行性，能够在源设备可用时导出受保护的凭证状态，并恢复至后续获取的硬件，且不克隆完整认证器或不向常规桌面软件暴露明文私钥。

Conclusion: 该方案填补了硬件认证器恢复能力的空白，但原型既非正式的安全证明，也非提议的最终标准，仍需进一步的形式化验证和标准化工作。

Abstract: Hardware authenticators deliberately resist private-key extraction, yet replacement, disaster recovery, and controlled migration create a legitimate need for portability. Existing guidance for device-bound credentials commonly reduces recovery risk by registering an additional authenticator before failure. That creates an independent credential registration and requires replacement hardware to exist in advance; it is redundancy, not a backup of the original credential. This paper addresses the resulting recovery gap by exporting protected credential state while the source is available and restoring it to hardware acquired later, without cloning a complete authenticator or exposing plaintext private keys to routine desktop software. We propose Vaulted Passkeys, a device-bound architecture in which a random 256-bit Kvault protects authenticated PKV1 credential envelopes through HKDF-separated keys and four explicit AEAD profiles. The design separates enrollment from export/import and the required vault from optional identity. We contribute a role-separated system model, wire format, threat analysis, implementation mapping, and falsifiable evaluation plan. The prototype demonstrates feasibility but is neither a formal security proof nor a proposed final standard.

</details>


### [163] [TLF: Rapid Characterization of RF Transceiver Parameters in Embedded Systems via Bus-Level Interception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13815)
*Larry Hernandez,Sergey Bratus*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TLF（收发器提升框架），一种从微控制器与收发器IC之间的总线级迹线中恢复RF收发器配置和运行时行为的工具。它通过有状态协议解码器重建RF参数、密钥材料和跳频序列，并在两个Semtech器件（SX1233和SX1276）上验证，可在数秒内从单次总线捕获中恢复完整的RF配置，足以配置匹配接收器或开发针对性的对抗措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要从硬件层面恢复RF收发器的配置和运行时行为，而不依赖于对目标固件的先验知识，以便配置匹配接收器或开发针对性的对抗措施。现有方法缺乏对总线级迹线的系统化解码能力。

Method: TLF包含一个有状态的协议解码器，针对收发器的寄存器和数据接口构建，从拦截的寄存器写入和FIFO传输中重建RF工作参数和行为。对于通过主机接口加载硬件加密密钥的总线连接收发器，密钥材料也可恢复。对于固件驱动的跳频（硬件辅助引擎或自定义调度），解码器提取信道表、跳频序列和时序。此外，可插拔的协议解码器将恢复的FIFO负载解释为应用PDU。

Result: 在两个不同Semtech系列的目标上评估：SX1233基于UAV C2调制解调器（采用固件级FHSS和逐包同步字轮换）和SX1276基于Meshtastic节点（采用LoRa寄存器覆盖）。从单次总线捕获中，TLF在数秒内恢复完整的寄存器暴露RF配置（调制、频带规划、相位行为），无需固件先验知识，并端到端演示了Meshtastic上的应用PDU解码。

Conclusion: TLF能从单次总线捕获中快速恢复RF收发器的寄存器级配置和行为，包括调制参数、频带规划、密钥材料和跳频序列。其恢复深度受限于收发器寄存器接口：完全在固件中实现的参数（自定义FEC、白化、加密）仅能作为不透明的FIFO负载观察。该方法需要物理访问或目标硬件仿真。

Abstract: We present TLF (Transceiver Lifter Framework), a tool for recovering RF transceiver configuration and runtime behavior from bus-level traces captured between a microcontroller and its transceiver IC. A stateful protocol decoder, built against the transceiver's register and data interface, reconstructs operating RF parameters and behavior from intercepted register writes and FIFO transfers. For bus-attached transceivers whose hardware-cryptography keys are loaded through the intercepted host interface, key material is also recoverable. Where the firmware drives frequency hopping -- either through a hardware-assisted engine or a custom schedule -- the decoder extracts the channel table, hop sequence, and timing. We evaluate the approach on two targets from different Semtech families: an SX1233-based UAV C2 modem employing firmware-level FHSS with per-packet sync word rotation, and an SX1276-based Meshtastic node exercising the LoRa register overlay. From a single bus capture, processed in seconds, TLF recovers the complete register-exposed RF configuration (modulation, band plan, phase behavior) without prior knowledge of the target firmware -- sufficient to configure a matched receiver or develop targeted countermeasures. Above the chip layer, a pluggable protocol decoder interprets recovered FIFO payloads as application PDUs, demonstrated end-to-end on Meshtastic. Firmware-level cryptographic state remains, as expected, opaque. The approach requires physical access or emulation of the target hardware, and its recovery depth is bounded by the transceiver's register interface: parameters implemented entirely in firmware (custom FEC, whitening, encryption) are observable only as opaque FIFO payloads.

</details>


### [164] [Verified Pythagorean Composition for Adaptive Cryptographic Games: Noise Flooding in Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13846)
*Yi Lee,Alexandru Cojocaru,Junyi Liu,Xiaodi Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文使用 Rocq（Coq）和 SSProve 形式化验证了噪声泛滥（noise flooding）对近似同态加密的抗解密攻击安全性，给出了 q-查询 IND-CPAD 敌手攻击概率的上界，避免了传统混合论证中线性损失，仅产生平方根损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 噪声泛滥是防御近似同态加密解密攻击的标准手段，但其安全性证明对组合极其敏感：若用普通混合论证，每次替换自适应解密回答都会在线性规模上累积损失；而密码学证明需累积条件 KL 散度并在最后一次性转化为统计距离，只产生参数关键的平方根损失。这种精细论证难以手工验证，因此需要机器验证。

Method: 在 Rocq 和 SSProve 中形式化证明。前提是任意近似正确且 IND-CPA 安全的 FHE 方案，对每个 q-查询 IND-CPAD 敌手构造归约。核心创新是构建了一个基于 SSProve 语义的新关系程序逻辑：其"毕达哥拉斯判定"（Pythagorean judgment）能够在不转化为统计距离的情况下组合条件 KL 预算；同时提供一个经过验证的迹编译器，将局部预言机规则提升为任意自适应程序上的规则，仅需一次最终转换。

Result: 形式化证明了不等式：Pr[IND-CPAD_NF^A=1] ≤ β_CPA(B_{A,q}) + √(qn)/(2γ)，其中 n 为明文维度，γ 为泛滥宽度乘子。该上界实现了平方根损失而非线性损失。

Conclusion: 通过新的关系程序逻辑和验证的迹编译器，成功在证明助手中复现了密码学文献中的精细组合论证，为近似同态加密噪声泛滥安全性的机器验证提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Noise flooding is a standard defense against decryption attacks on approximate homomorphic encryption, but its security proof is unusually sensitive to composition. Replacing each of $q$ adaptive decryption answers with a statistically close simulation and applying an ordinary hybrid argument loses linearly in $q$. The cryptographic proof instead accumulates conditional Kullback-Leibler (KL) costs and converts to statistical distance once, giving the parameter-critical square-root loss.
  We machine-check this argument using Rocq and SSProve. Given any fully homomorphic encryption scheme that is approximately correct and IND-CPA secure, we formalize a reduction for every $q$-query IND-CPAD adversary and prove \[
  \Pr[\mathsf{IND\text{-}CPAD}_{\mathsf{NF}}^{\mathcal A}=1]
  \leq β_{\mathsf{CPA}}(\mathcal B_{\mathcal A,q})
  + \frac{\sqrt{qn}}{2γ}. \] where $n$ is the plaintext dimension and $γ$ is the flooding-width multiplier. Our proof constructs a new relational program logic over SSProve semantics. Its Pythagorean judgment composes conditional KL budgets without converting them to statistical distance, and a verified trace compiler lifts a local oracle rule to arbitrary adaptive programs with a single final conversion.

</details>


### [165] [CipherSight: Robust Website Fingerprinting via Record-Resource Semantic Supervision under Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13905)
*Runhan Song,Qiqi Liu,Chuanzhou Pan,Zhenquan Ding,Youquan Xian,Chongru Fan,Lei Cui,Wei Wang,Zhiyu Hao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CipherSight是一个基于TLS记录的层次化框架，用于鲁棒的HTTPS网站指纹识别，通过捕获TLS记录级属性和层次化结构模式来应对分布漂移和开放世界场景下的性能退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WF方法主要从原始TCP数据包序列中学习，对传输层伪影敏感，难以捕获稳定且可泛化的网站表示，在实际部署中面临时间和地理分布漂移导致的OOD问题以及开放世界中的未见网站，导致性能显著下降。

Method: CipherSight从TLS记录而非TCP数据包中学习网站表示，联合编码多个记录级属性；采用层次化架构同时捕获流内TLS记录依赖和跨并发流的流间交互；使用掩码记录建模(MRM)任务学习上下文流量语义，并通过细粒度记录-资源标注作为特权监督，结合结构感知目标和语义蒸馏来学习鲁棒表示。

Result: 在封闭世界超过2000个网站类别下达到95.41%准确率，在时间和地理漂移场景下保持90%以上的准确率，全面优于所有评估的基线方法。

Conclusion: CipherSight通过从TCP数据包转向TLS记录并利用层次化架构与自监督/特权监督策略，有效提升了HTTPS WF在分布漂移和开放世界条件下的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: HTTPS website fingerprinting (WF) aims to identify visited websites from metadata observable in encrypted traffic. However, real-world deployments introduce a significant out-of-distribution (OOD) problem caused by temporal and geographic changes, while previously unseen websites are common in open-world scenarios. Existing methods primarily learn from raw TCP packet sequences and struggle to capture stable and generalizable website representations, resulting in performance degradation under practical conditions.
  We propose CipherSight, a TLS-record-based hierarchical framework for robust HTTPS WF. Unlike existing approaches that rely on TCP packet sequences and are sensitive to transport-layer artifacts, CipherSight learns website representations from TLS records by jointly encoding multiple record-level attributes. It introduces a hierarchical architecture that captures both intra-flow dependencies among TLS records and inter-flow interactions across concurrent flows, enabling the model to exploit structural patterns in HTTPS traffic. Besides, to learn robust representations, CipherSight employs a masked record modeling (MRM) task to capture contextual traffic semantics and leverages fine-grained record-resource annotations as privileged supervision through structure-aware objectives and semantic distillation. Experiments show that CipherSight achieves 95.41% accuracy across more than 2,000 website classes in the closed-world setting and maintains over 90% accuracy under both temporal and geographic drift, consistently outperforming all evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [166] [Characterizing the Variance Envelope: A Multi-Dimensional Analysis of Spectre Telemetry Across Architectures and Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13920)
*Jaya Keshava Chandra Kotha,Jean-Luc Gaudiot*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究硬件攻击（如Spectre）在HPC指标中的"方差包络"（即攻击签名在不同环境下如何偏移的全范围），跨Intel、ARM、AMD三种架构，测试三种攻击变体、四种节奏模式和四种背景噪声条件。结论证明HPC签名的脆弱性，暴露AMD Jaguar上Prime+Probe攻击的微架构级持久失败，并呼吁采用架构感知的自适应监控而非静态模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习检测HPC异常的方法在受控环境中有效，但静态模型在真实世界面对背景系统噪声、多样化攻击变体和对抗性流量节奏时会失效。作者旨在通过刻画攻击签名在异构架构下的完整变化范围（"方差包络"），揭示静态检测模型的不足，为可靠的运行时检测提供基础。

Method: 作者构建了广泛的实验矩阵，涵盖三种攻击变体（包括Spectre类别）、四种流量节奏模式和四种背景噪声条件，并在Intel、ARM和AMD三种架构上运行实验，系统性测量和分析HPC指标中攻击签名的漂移情况。此外，针对AMD Jaguar平台进行微架构级分析，验证Prime+Probe攻击在该平台上的行为特征。

Result: 实验证明HPC签名高度脆弱，极易因执行环境改变而发生扭曲。同时发现了关键性微架构瓶颈：Prime+Probe攻击在AMD Jaguar上存在持久的硬件级失败。这证明了静态模型无法可靠地跨架构和跨环境检测硬件攻击。

Conclusion: 可靠的运行时硬件攻击检测需要架构感知的自适应监控方案，而非依赖静态模型。这一结论为未来设计更鲁棒的硬件安全监控系统指明了方向。

Abstract: Hardware attacks like Spectre exploit built-in processor vulnerabilities, leaving anomalous footprints in Hardware Performance Counter (HPC) metrics. While machine learning can detect these footprints in controlled settings, static models fail in the real world when confronted with background system noise, diverse attack variants, and adversarial traffic pacing. To close this gap, this paper characterizes the "variance envelope"-the full range of how attack signatures shift- across Intel, ARM, and AMD architectures. We evaluate an extensive experimental matrix encompassing three attack variants, four pacing modes, and four background-noise conditions. Our analysis proves that HPC signatures are highly fragile and easily warped by their execution environment. Furthermore, we expose a critical microarchitectural bottleneck: the persistent, hardware-level failure of Prime+Probe attacks on the AMD Jaguar. Ultimately, this comprehensive characterization demonstrates that reliable runtime detection requires architecture-aware, adaptive monitoring rather than static models.

</details>


### [167] [CoSA: Context-Aware Severity Assessment via Context Analysis with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13928)
*Jinfeng Jiang,Yikun Li,Chengran Yang,Ting Zhang,Wen Bin Leow,Yide Yin,Eng Lieh Ouh,Lwin Khin Shar,David Lo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CoSA是一种上下文感知的漏洞严重性评估方法，通过构建代码属性图并结合两阶段仓库级剪枝策略（静态剪枝+智能体LLM引导剪枝），检索CVSS相关的仓库级证据，将上下文浓缩为面向CVSS指标的文字摘要，再输入轻量级Transformer预测器进行指标推断。在包含6,816个CVSS标注实例、覆盖90种CWE类型的仓库级数据集上，CoSA相较最强的基线在准确率上提升14.4%，Macro-F1提升15.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动评估CVSS基础指标耗时费力，而现有自动化方法往往无法捕获仓库级（repository-level）证据，导致许多CVSS基础指标的评估不准确。仓库感知评估的挑战在于相关证据分散在整个仓库中且夹杂大量噪声。

Method: CoSA采用三步流程：(1) 构建代码属性图（CPG）作为仓库的结构化表示；(2) 应用两阶段仓库剪枝策略——先用轻量级静态剪枝保留结构上邻近的上下文，再用智能体LLM引导的剪枝步骤保留CVSS相关上下文并收集支撑证据；(3) 由LLM将检索到的仓库上下文整合为紧凑的、面向各CVSS指标的文字摘要，输入轻量级Transformer预测器推断CVSS基础指标。同时构建了一个覆盖90种CWE类型、包含6,816个CVSS标注实例的高质量仓库级数据集。

Result: 在真实世界漏洞上的实验表明，CoSA一致优于函数级（function-level）和纯LLM基线。相较最佳基线，预测准确率提升14.4%，Macro-F1提升15.3%。

Conclusion: 显式、面向指标（metric-oriented）的仓库级上下文检索对于实用且可靠的自动化漏洞严重性评估至关重要，CoSA验证了这一范式的有效性，为后续仓库感知的CVSS评估研究提供了方向和数据基础。

Abstract: Accurate vulnerability severity assessment is essential for prioritizing remediation, yet manually assessing Common Vulnerability Scoring System (CVSS) base metrics remains labor-intensive. Existing automated approaches often fail to capture the repository-level evidence required for assessing many CVSS base metrics. Such repository-aware assessment is challenging because relevant evidence is scattered across the entire repository under heavy noise.
  To address these challenges, we present CoSA, a Context-aware vulnerability Severity Assessment approach that infers CVSS base metrics from repository artifacts. CoSA constructs a code property graph (CPG) and applies a two-stage repository-pruning strategy: lightweight static pruning to preserve structurally proximal context, followed by an agentic large language model (LLM)-guided pruning step to retain CVSS-relevant context while collecting supporting evidence. The LLM then consolidates the retrieved repository context into compact, CVSS metric-wise textual summaries, which are fed into a lightweight transformer predictor. We also construct a higher-quality repository-level dataset comprising 6,816 CVSS labeled instances spanning 90 Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) types.
  Experiments on real-world vulnerabilities show that CoSA consistently outperforms function-level and pure-LLM baselines. It improves prediction accuracy by 14.4% and Macro-F1 by 15.3% over the best-performing baseline, suggesting that explicit, metric-oriented repository context retrieval is crucial for practical and reliable automated severity assessment.

</details>


### [168] [Extracting and Verifying Illicit Bitcoin Addresses from Underground Forum Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13930)
*Abdoul Nasser Hassane Amadou,Arnaud Legout,Imane Fouad,Konstantin Avrachenkov,Anas Motii*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个可复现的流水线，从地下网络犯罪论坛 HackForums 构建有证据支持的比特币地址标签数据集，包含 2,438 个经人工验证的非法比特币地址，并附有上下文证据和链上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有比特币标签数据集多来自社区举报、区块链启发式规则、事件专项收集或专有标注流程，其构建方法难以公开复现，且很少提供地址直接参与非法活动的证据，导致研究可信度和可验证性不足。

Method: 该流水线结合三个环节：(1) 利用 LLM 辅助筛查从 HackForums 十五年间的归档讨论中识别涉及非法交易的比特币地址；(2) 专家评审验证筛选结果；(3) 链上验证确认地址确实参与了相关交易。每个标签均附带地下论坛讨论的上下文证据及链上验证信息。

Result: 构建了包含 2,438 个经人工验证的非法比特币地址数据集，时间跨度覆盖 2010-2024 年，涵盖 LLM 筛查阶段标注的十二类网络犯罪类别。数据集、时间元数据和完整提取流水线均公开发布。

Conclusion: 该工作通过可复现的流水线和证据支持的标注方式，弥补了现有比特币非法地址数据集的构建透明度不足和证据缺失问题，为加密货币相关网络犯罪的复现性研究提供了新的数据基础。

Abstract: Existing labeled Bitcoin datasets are largely derived from community-reported abuse, blockchain heuristics, incident-specific collections, or proprietary labeling processes. Their construction methods are rarely publicly reproducible and often provide limited evidence that an address was directly involved in illicit activity. We present a reproducible pipeline for constructing evidence-backed Bitcoin labels from HackForums, an underground cybercrime forum with fifteen years of archived activity. The pipeline combines LLM-assisted screening, expert review, and on-chain validation to identify Bitcoin addresses explicitly associated with illicit transactions discussed on the forum. Each released label is supported by contextual evidence from underground discussions and validated on-chain. The resulting dataset contains 2,438 manually verified illicit Bitcoin addresses spanning 2010-2024 and twelve cybercrime categories assigned during LLM screening. We release the dataset, temporal metadata, and the complete extraction pipeline to support reproducible research on cryptocurrency-facilitated cybercrime.

</details>


### [169] [Vectorized SQIsign Implementation Using AVX-512](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13948)
*Weize Wang,Chutong Wang,Yu Wu,Qifan Xue,Jieyu Zheng,Yunlei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出基于AVX-512 IFMA指令集的SQIsign完整向量化实现，通过系统性地重新设计素数域、扩展域、椭圆曲线及配对运算的计算栈，在NIST安全等级I下实现了签名2.69倍、验证3.18倍的加速，并论证了该优化技术在未来AVX10架构中的长期可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SQIsign是NIST后量子密码标准化进程中唯一的同源类数字签名方案，拥有紧凑的密钥和签名尺寸，但其签名过程计算密集，实际部署受到严重制约。此前缺乏针对SQIsign的全面向量化（SIMD）优化研究，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者利用AVX-512 IFMA指令集，对SQIsign的计算栈进行了系统性重构，涵盖四个层面：(1)素数域与扩展域算术；(2)椭圆曲线运算，包括批处理点加倍与标量乘法；(3)配对计算（cubical arithmetic）；(4)二维同源求值。同时结合Qlapoti技术进一步提升性能。

Result: 在NIST安全等级I下，相比参考实现取得了显著的性能提升：签名速度提升2.69倍，验证速度提升3.18倍。此外，作者澄清了AVX-512已过时的误解，指出Intel AVX10架构（10.2修订版，预计2026年底广泛部署）将在所有P核和E核上标准化IFMA指令。

Conclusion: SIMD向量化是同源类后量子密码方案中一个关键但尚未充分探索的优化维度，其独立于算法层面的最新进展。本文的向量化策略具有架构无关性，可作为方法论基础推广到更广泛的同源类密码构造中。

Abstract: SQIsign is the sole isogeny-based digital signature scheme submitted to the NIST Post-Quantum Cryptography standardization process, distinguished by its foundation on the hardness of the endomorphism ring problem for supersingular elliptic curves. While offering compact key and signature sizes, SQIsign's practical deployment is hindered by computationally intensive signing procedures. This paper presents the first comprehensive vectorized implementation of SQIsign utilizing the AVX-512 Integer Fused Multiply-Add (IFMA) instruction set architecture. By systematically redesigning the computational stack---encompassing prime-field and extension-field arithmetic, elliptic curve operations including batched point doubling and scalar multiplication, as well as pairing computations via cubical arithmetic and two-dimensional isogeny evaluations---we achieve substantial performance improvements over the reference implementation. When combined with Qlapoti technology, our implementation attains a $2.69\times$ speedup for signing and a $3.18\times$ improvement for verification at NIST security level I.
  Contrary to misconceptions regarding the obsolescence of AVX-512, we emphasize that Intel's AVX10 instruction set architecture (revision 10.2, scheduled for widespread deployment in late 2026) will standardize AVX-512 capabilities---including IFMA instructions---across both performance and efficiency cores, ensuring long-term viability of these optimization techniques. Furthermore, our vectorization strategies are architecture-agnostic and provide a methodological foundation applicable to broader isogeny-based cryptographic constructions. This work demonstrates that SIMD vectorization represents a critical yet underexplored optimization dimension for post-quantum isogeny-based schemes, independent of recent algorithmic advances.

</details>


### [170] [Structural Leakage in Graph Encryption: Attacks and Defenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13981)
*Hua Shen,Renzhi Chen,Ge Wu,Willy Susilo,Jing Chen,Mingwu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了图加密方案（GES）在单对最短路径（SPSP）查询中的结构泄漏问题，先揭示PathGES在重度分解（HLD）中存在超过99%的一对一token-路径映射等结构失衡，可被Falzon-Paterson攻击和侧信道推断利用；进而提出Fragment Tree攻击在稀疏图上实现高达10.24%的精确查询恢复；最后提出BlindGES增强方案，通过合并-分割机制和两级多映射索引将一对一映射降至20%以下，同时减少50%设置时间、32%存储开销，并将路径长度泄漏限制在1%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图加密方案支持安全外包图数据并高效执行查询，但现有方案（如PathGES）在SPSP查询中存在严重的结构性泄漏，使攻击者可能恢复查询内容。已有研究对此类泄漏的系统性分析不足，亟需全面评估攻击面并提出更安全的防御方案。

Method: 本文采用综合分析框架：1）对PathGES进行结构泄漏分析，揭示HLD分解中的一对多/一对一映射失衡问题；2）提出Fragment Tree攻击方法，利用上述结构弱点恢复查询内容；3）设计BlindGES防御方案，引入Merge-and-Divide机制和两级多映射索引以减少一对一映射、降低泄漏；4）在七个真实世界数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: PathGES在真实数据集上超过99%的token-路径映射为一对一映射；Fragment Tree攻击在稀疏图上实现最高10.24%的精确查询恢复；BlindGES将一对一映射降至20%以下，设置时间减少50%，存储开销减少32%，路径长度泄漏控制在1%以内。

Conclusion: 现有GES方案存在显著的结构泄漏风险，攻击者可利用HLD分解不平衡等弱点恢复查询内容；BlindGES通过Merge-and-Divide机制和两级多映射索引，在保持查询效率的同时有效缓解结构泄漏，为SPSP查询下的图加密方案提供了更安全的设计方向。

Abstract: Graph encryption schemes (GES) enable secure outsourcing of graph data while supporting efficient queries. This report provides a comprehensive analysis of structural leakage in GES for single-pair shortest path (SPSP) queries, integrating findings from two recent works. First, we analyze PathGES, a scheme designed to resist query recovery attacks through heavy-light decomposition (HLD) and canonical fragment encoding. Our analysis reveals that PathGES suffers from significant imbalances in HLD decomposition, with over 99% of token-path mappings being one-to-one on real-world datasets, enabling both the Falzon-Paterson attack and side-channel inference of path lengths. Second, we present Fragment Tree attack that exploits these structural weaknesses to recover query contents, achieving up to 10.24% exact recovery on sparse graphs. Third, we introduce BlindGES, an enhanced scheme incorporating a Merge-and-Divide mechanism and two-level multimap index that reduces one-to-one mappings to below 20%, cuts setup time by 50%, reduces storage overhead by 32%, and limits path length leakage to under 1%. This report systematically presents attack methodologies, defense mechanisms, security proofs, and experimental evaluations on seven real-world datasets.

</details>


### [171] [P2Skill: Privacy Preserving Skill Distillation for Cloud-Local LLM Inference Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14094)
*Myunghoon Ryu,Geunpyo Park,Sungjoon Lee,XinYu Piao,Jong-Kook Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: P2Skill是一种基于提示词技能蒸馏的隐私保护LLM推理方法，让本地小语言模型(SLM)通过技能提示自主完成分解、PII感知路由、改写和重建，在无需隐私微调或辅助检测器的情况下，实现云端大模型的推理能力与本地数据隐私保护的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云-本地LLM推理系统需要利用云端大模型的推理能力，同时保护个人设备上的敏感用户数据。现有的隐私保护方法（如提示扰动、实体掩码、模型微调）存在上下文语义扭曲或需要额外训练成本的问题，亟需一种既无需针对性微调又能保持语义完整性的隐私保护方案。

Method: P2Skill提出提示词驱动的技能蒸馏方法：本地SLM通过遵循技能提示自主执行四个步骤——分解（decomposition）、PII感知路由（PII-aware routing）、改写（paraphrasing）和重建（reconstruction）。技能由云端LLM根据执行失败案例进行迭代精炼，使本地SLM能够超越对已记忆PII模式的依赖实现泛化，完全不需要隐私专用微调或学习型辅助检测器。

Result: 在四领域基准测试上，P2Skill的隐私保护推理质量比先前基线方法分别提升了1.69倍和3.66倍，显著优于现有方案。

Conclusion: P2Skill验证了基于提示词技能蒸馏的隐私保护方法在云-本地LLM推理场景中的有效性，以零训练成本实现了更好的隐私保护推理质量，为隐私敏感的LLM推理提供了一条无需额外训练或辅助模型的新路径。

Abstract: Cloud-local LLM inference systems have the potential to use the reasoning capability of large cloud models while protecting sensitive user data on personal devices. Cloud-bound requests must exclude personally identifiable information (PII) to prevent external data leakage. Existing privacy-preserving methods rely on prompt perturbation, entity masking, or model fine-tuning, but these approaches may distort contextual semantics or require additional training. This paper proposes P2Skill, a prompt-based skill distillation method in which a local small language model (SLM) autonomously performs decomposition, PII-aware routing, paraphrasing, and reconstruction by following the skill prompts. Skills are iteratively refined from execution failures by a cloud LLM, enabling the local SLM to generalize beyond memorized PII patterns, and therefore P2Skill requires no privacy-specific fine-tuning or learned auxiliary detectors. Evaluation on a four-domain benchmark shows that P2Skill achieves $1.69\times$ and $3.66\times$ higher privacy-preserved inference quality than previous baselines.

</details>


### [172] [BGA: A noise-immune neural distillation framework for malicious signature extraction in high-entropy encrypted flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14126)
*Sheng Hong,Yixuan Huang,Weiwei Jiang,Junyuan Zhang,Jiacheng Wang,Ruijian Jiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出BGA框架，一种抗噪的神经蒸馏框架，用于加密威胁情报检测。通过ANOVA解耦高判别性控制面特征（工业设定点）与加密噪声，结合WGAN-GP解决类别不平衡问题，并集成BiLSTM与自适应门控多头注意力机制来抑制加密伪影、放大恶意签名。在CIC-IDS-2018和Edge-IIoT基准上关键指标均超过95.2%，推理延迟仅0.2820ms，适合工业边缘网关实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高熵TLS 1.3加密流量中存在注意力稀释问题，传统方法难以从随机加密噪声中有效提取恶意信号，且工业控制场景中罕见攻击样本（如恶意状态命令注入MSCI）的检测困难，需要一种抗噪且能处理极端类别不平衡的深度学习框架。

Method: 1) 利用ANOVA分析将高判别性控制面特征（工业设定点）从随机加密噪声中解耦；2) 集成WGAN-GP（带梯度惩罚、满足1-Lipschitz约束）合成高保真少数类样本以解决极端类别不平衡；3) 核心架构采用BiLSTM提取时序依赖，并设计自适应门控多头注意力机制作为神经滤波器，动态抑制加密伪影、增强恶意特征；4) 采用神经蒸馏策略增强噪声鲁棒性。

Result: 在CIC-IDS-2018和Edge-IIoT基准上，所有关键指标超过95.2%；MSCI罕见攻击检测召回率提升43.2%；噪声注入压力测试中相比vanilla Transformer有8.57%的性能优势；推理延迟0.2820ms（ARM端理论扩展估计1.6920ms），验证了异构工业边缘网关实时部署的可行性。

Conclusion: BGA为加密工业流量的威胁检测提供了一种高效且抗噪的解决方案，兼具检测精度与实时推理能力，可作为未来硬件实现（如工业边缘网关部署）的架构基线。但摘要未给出跨不同TLS版本、真实部署环境下的泛化性验证细节，是该方向后续可探索的空间。

Abstract: To mitigate attention dilution in high-entropy TLS 1.3 flows, we propose BGA, a noise-immune neural distillation framework for encrypted threat intelligence.The methodology first employs Analysis of Variance (ANOVA) to decouple high-discriminatory control-plane features - specifically industrial setpoints - from stochastic cryptographic noise. To resolve the extreme class imbalance within a corpus of 86,878 flow records, a Wasserstein GAN with Gradient Penalty (WGAN-GP) module, enforcing the 1-Lipschitz constraint, is integrated to synthesize high-fidelity minority samples, elevating the detection recall of rare Malicious State Command Injections(MSCI) attacks by 43.2%. At its core, the BGA architecture integrates Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) for temporal dependency extraction and an Adaptive Gated Multi-Head Attention mechanism. This gated unit functions as a neural filter to dynamically suppress encryption artifacts while amplifying malicious signatures. Extensive evaluations on CIC-IDS-2018 and Edge-IIoT benchmarks demonstrate a performance ceiling exceeding 95.2% across all key metrics. Furthermore, noise-injection stress tests confirm BGAs superior structural resilience with a 8.57% performance margin over vanilla Transformers, while its ultra-low inference latency of 0.2820 ms (estimated 1.6920 ms via theoretical scaling for ARM) indicates a high potential for real-time feasibility on heterogeneous industrial edge gateways, providing a promising architectural baseline for future hardware implementation.

</details>


### [173] [MazeRunner: Nonlinear Task and Clue Orchestration for LLM-driven Black-Box Automated Penetration Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14216)
*Zhenyuan Li,Yi Jiang,Junjie Cheng,Yaokun Li,Jing Qiu,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MazeRunner 是一个基于三智能体任务与线索编排框架的自主渗透测试系统，通过全局编排、上下文密集执行和失败导向审查的分离设计，解决真实世界黑盒测试中攻击图未知、观测多分支、失败模糊和长跨度线索等问题，在10个HTB目标上以Claude Sonnet 4.5完成47.7%的标注子任务，优于PentestGPT-V2（36.2%）和Claude Code（34.2%），并在6个目标上获得用户级及以上访问权限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的渗透测试代理主要在简化的线性场景中执行端到端工作流，而真实世界的黑盒测试本质上是非线性的：攻击图初始未知，需要从环境反馈中逐步推断；观测可能揭示多个攻击分支，失败常常是模糊的，关键线索可能跨越很长的行动时间跨度。这导致现有代理容易陷入深度优先探索、误诊失败并遗忘先前的证据。

Method: MazeRunner 采用三智能体任务与线索编排框架，将全局编排、上下文密集型执行和失败导向审查进行分离，同时持续维护任务状态和环境证据。该设计支持行动修正、前提恢复、分支切换和长距离线索关联。

Result: 在10个近期发布的HTB目标上评估，每个系统-目标运行限制为2000万LLM token并防止目标特定解决方案泄露。使用Claude Sonnet 4.5时，MazeRunner完成47.7%的标注子任务，优于PentestGPT-V2（36.2%）和Claude Code（34.2%）。实现了6个目标的用户级或更高级别访问（包括2个目标的root访问），而同模型基线仅在2个目标上达到用户级访问且从未获得root权限。执行轨迹分析表明MazeRunner探索更多的攻击分支并以更高效率获取shell。

Conclusion: MazeRunner通过三智能体编排框架有效解决了黑盒渗透测试中的非线性攻击图推断、模糊失败处理和长跨度证据关联等核心挑战，在任务完成率和系统入侵深度上均显著优于现有基线，验证了任务与线索分离编排在复杂安全审计场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Penetration testing is essential yet resource-intensive. Although large language models (LLMs) show promise for automating security auditing, existing agents mainly execute end-to-end workflows in simplified linear scenarios. Real-world black-box testing is fundamentally nonlinear: the attack graph is initially unknown and must be incrementally inferred from environmental feedback. Observations may reveal multiple attack branches, failures are often ambiguous, and critical clues may span long action horizons. Existing agents therefore tend to become trapped in depth-first exploration, misdiagnose failures, and forget prior evidence. We present MazeRunner, an autonomous penetration testing system built on a three-agent task-and-clue orchestration framework. It separates global orchestration, context-intensive execution, and failure-oriented review while persistently maintaining task states and environmental evidence. This design supports action revision, prerequisite recovery, branch switching, and long-range clue correlation. We evaluate MazeRunner on 10 recently released HTB targets, limiting each system-target run to 20 million LLM tokens and preventing target-specific solution leakage. With Claude Sonnet 4.5, MazeRunner completes 47.7% of annotated subtasks, compared with 36.2% for PentestGPT-V2 and 34.2% for Claude Code. It achieves user-level or higher access on six targets, including root access on two; each same-model baseline reaches user-level access on only two targets and never obtains root access. Execution-trace analysis further shows that MazeRunner explores more attack branches and acquires shells more efficiently.

</details>


### [174] [A Four-Axis Trustworthiness Benchmark for LLM-as-Judge in Principle-Based Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14329)
*Dipankar Sarkar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出原则性监管（如FCA的"公平、清晰、不误导"等评价性标准）中的LLM-as-judge必须从四个维度评估：准确性、释义鲁棒性、对抗鲁棒性和校准性。作者发布了Principle-Bench基准（168个加密资产金融推广场景）及Ceca评估器，实验表明没有任何单一方法在所有维度上占优，且120B大模型在关键词填充攻击下准确率暴跌47个点，揭示了"合规剧场"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原则性监管使用如"公平、清晰、不误导"或"交付良好结果"等评价性标准，这些标准无法简化为二元谓词，因此LLM-as-judge正被越来越多地用作替代方案。但现有工作缺乏对这类评判模型系统性的多维评估框架，需要建立覆盖鲁棒性和校准性的综合评价体系。

Method: 作者提出四轴评估框架（准确性、释义鲁棒性、对抗鲁棒性、校准性），发布Principle-Bench基准：包含168个加密资产金融推广场景，映射到两个UK FCA原则，并在预注册规则下构造了释义变体、对抗性关键词填充和边界扰动。同时提出Ceca（Calibrated Exemplar-Cluster Assessment），一个带校准、可审计的评估器，能输出精确的逐示例反事实归因。实验比较了关键词计数、三种sentence-transformer嵌入器、一个开源权重LLM评判器及校准级联。

Result: 没有任何方法在四个轴上全面占优。120B LLM评判器在良性输入上表现最强，但在关键词填充的Consumer Duty输入上准确率从0.74暴跌至0.27（损失47个点），呈现"合规剧场"现象。另一个不同模型族的评判器在同一分割上仅有Cohen's kappa=0.16的一致性，将失败定位到模型本身而非语料库。

Conclusion: 任何面向部署的原则性监管LLM评判器，除了总体准确率外，必须报告逐原则的对抗欺骗率和事后校准指标。Ceca提供了校准且可审计的评估路径，但总体结论是：当前方法均不满足部署级要求，需要方法论上的进一步突破。

Abstract: Principle-based regulation, with evaluative standards such as "fair, clear, and not misleading" or "deliver good outcomes", cannot be reduced to binary predicates, and LLM-as-judge is increasingly used as the substitute. Our position is that any such judge must be evaluated on four axes: accuracy, paraphrase robustness, adversarial robustness, and calibration. We release Principle-Bench, 168 cryptoasset financial-promotion scenarios mapped to two UK FCA principles, with paraphrase, adversarial keyword-stuffing, and boundary perturbations authored under a pre-registered rubric; the first benchmark covering all four axes for principle-based regulation. We also introduce Ceca (Calibrated Exemplar-Cluster Assessment): a calibrated, auditable assessor that emits exact per-exemplar counterfactual attributions. Across keyword counting, three sentence-transformer embedders, an open-weight LLM-judge, and a calibrated cascade, no method dominates all four axes. A 120B LLM-judge, strongest on benign inputs, loses 47 accuracy points (0.74 to 0.27) on keyword-stuffed Consumer Duty inputs: "compliance theatre." A second judge from a different model family agrees only at Cohen's kappa = 0.16 on that split, localising the failure to the model rather than the corpus. Any deployment-grade LLM-judge for principle-based regulation must report per-principle adversarial deception and post-hoc calibration alongside aggregate accuracy.

</details>


### [175] [A Hybrid LLM-Based Framework for Automated Security Annotation Generation in Business Process Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14370)
*Md Kamrul Islam,Tiphaine Henry,Mattia Salnitri,Julius Köpke,Sami Souihi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个混合框架，结合LLM语义提取、模式约束映射、规则归一化和确定性验证，自动从BPMN流程模型和安全需求文档生成符合SecBPMN2规范的注释。在27个跨域流程模型上的评估表明，与人类分析师相比，该方法在保持可比召回率的条件下显著提高了精确率（0.58 vs 0.29），注释错误减少近50%，且生成速度大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从自然语言规范推导准确的BPMN安全注释目前仍依赖手工专家操作，过程繁琐且易出错。虽然已有SecBPMN2等BPMN扩展支持安全注释，但缺乏自动化的注释生成方法。

Method: 构建混合框架，包括：(1) 基于大语言模型的语义提取；(2) 基于模式约束（schema-constrained）的映射；(3) 基于规则的归一化处理；(4) 确定性验证。输入为BPMN流程模型和安全需求文档，输出为符合SecBPMN2规范的注释。

Result: 在27个跨域流程模型数据集上，框架生成注释在结构上有效且模式完整性高。相较人类分析师，精确率达0.58（人类0.29），召回率0.52（人类0.50），错误或错位注释减少近50%，且生成速度显著快于人工注释。

Conclusion: 混合LLM与规则自动化方法能有效降低建模工作量，同时提升一致性和可靠性，为安全-by-design的BPM提供可扩展的基础。

Abstract: The modelling and analysis of secure business processes require the incorporation of security annotations into process models. Although BPMN extensions, including SecBPMN2, exist for this purpose, the derivation of accurate and complete security annotations from natural-language specifications remains a manual, expert-intensive, and error-prone task. This paper presents a hybrid framework that takes a BPMN process model and a security requirements document as input and automatically generates security annotations adhering to the SecBPMN2 specification. The approach combines Large Language Model (LLM)--based semantic extraction with schema-constrained mapping, rule-based normalization, and deterministic validation. The framework is evaluated comprehensively on a curated dataset of 27 process models from various domains. The results indicate that it consistently produces structurally valid SecBPMN2 annotations with high schema completeness. Compared to human security analysts, the system achieves substantially higher precision (0.58 vs. 0.29) while maintaining comparable recall (0.52 vs. 0.50) and reduces erroneous or misplaced annotations by nearly 50%. In addition, annotation generation is significantly faster than manual annotation. These findings demonstrate that hybrid LLM- and rule-based automation can reduce modeling effort while improving consistency and reliability, thereby providing a scalable foundation for security-by-design BPM.

</details>


### [176] [STINER: Automated Extraction of Strategic Cyber Threat Intelligence from X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14418)
*Yasir Ech-Chammakhy,Oussama Azrara,Jaafar Chbili,Anas Motii*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出STINER，一个用于从社交媒体流中提取战略网络威胁情报(CTI)的专家标注语料库和细粒度实体类型体系。通过构建2,100条真实告警的标注数据集和8种实体类型，作者基准测试了9个模型（含12种配置），发现领域自适应编码器DarkBERT达到89.33%的严格F1分数，优于通用基线和微调LLM。基于STINER-DarkBERT的欧洲威胁态势分析（2025年上半年）与官方报告一致，并能比厂商报告更早捕获SafePay勒索软件活动的早期信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X（原Twitter）已成为获取战略CTI（如目标行业、勒索软件归属、数据损失规模）的最快来源，其实时泄露公告往往比正式厂商报告早数天。然而社交媒体语言高度非正式且不规则，传统NER模型难以解析，造成自动化防御系统的盲区。因此需要专门针对社交媒体的战略实体抽取方案。

Method: 提出了STINER（战略CTI NER）方法：首先构建一个包含2,100条真实告警的高质量专家标注语料库，设计涵盖Threat Actor、Sector、Location等8种围绕战略关键维度的细粒度实体类型体系。随后基准测试9个模型、12种配置，涵盖通用/领域自适应编码器、开放模式抽取以及零样本/微调生成式LLM，并基于最优模型（STINER-DarkBERT）开展欧洲威胁态势分析。

Result: 领域自适应编码器DarkBERT在严格F1上达到89.33%，同时优于通用基线和微调LLM，且推理延迟明显更低。基于STINER-DarkBERT的欧洲2025年上半年威胁态势分析与官方报告在主要目标上一致，同时揭示了西班牙攻击的独特可见性特征，并能比厂商回顾性报告更早地识别SafePay勒索软件活动的早期信号。

Conclusion: 社交媒体驱动的战略CTI抽取在准确性和时效性上均具有显著优势。领域自适应编码器（而非大型生成模型）是当前定位下性价比最优的技术路线；STINER语料库和实体体系为自动化威胁情报系统提供了一个实用且高效的解决方案，能够补充传统厂商报告的盲区。

Abstract: Strategic Cyber Threat Intelligence (CTI) focuses on high-level insights, such as identifying targeted industries, attributing attacks to specific ransomware groups, and assessing the scale of data loss. Today, X (formerly Twitter) has become the fastest source for this intelligence, often hosting real-time breach announcements days before formal vendor reports. Converting this raw chatter into actionable intelligence requires navigating a complex linguistic landscape. Conventional Named Entity Recognition (NER) models struggle to parse the informal and highly irregular dialect of social media, creating a blind spot for automated defense systems. To address this challenge, we introduce STINER, a taxonomy and expert-annotated corpus for extracting strategic intelligence from social media streams. We construct a high-quality, expert-annotated dataset of 2,100 real-world alerts and propose a granular taxonomy of eight entity types centered on strategic pivots such as Threat Actor, Sector, and Location. We benchmark nine models across 12 evaluated configurations, spanning general-purpose and domain-adapted encoders, open-schema extraction, and generative LLMs in both zero-shot and fine-tuned settings. Domain-adapted encoders such as DarkBERT reach a strict F1-score of 89.33%, outperforming both general-purpose baselines and fine-tuned Large Language Models, which additionally incur substantially higher inference latency. Leveraging STINER-DarkBERT, we conduct a European threat landscape analysis for H1 2025. Our results align with official reporting on major targets while highlighting the distinct visibility profile of attacks in Spain, and illustrate how social-media-driven extraction can surface early signals of the SafePay ransomware campaign prior to its retrospective characterization in vendor threat landscape reports.

</details>


### [177] [Lower Bounds on Black-Box Constructions of Pseudorandom Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14501)
*Bar Alon,Itai Dinur,Muthuramakrishnan Venkitasubramaniam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了从伪随机生成器(PRG)构造伪随机函数(PRF)的全黑盒构造的效率下界。结果表明，在特定约束下（构造和归约均为黑盒，且归约与敌手的交互次数独立于敌手调用其底层函数的次数），不存在只需要o(n/log n)或o(in/log in)次非自适应PRG调用的构造，其中in是PRF的输入长度。此不可能性对甚至只输出1比特的弱PRF也成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GGM构造是经典的黑盒PRF构造方案，但需要调用PRG ω(log n)次。至今没有已知的黑盒构造能使用更少的调用次数。Beimel等人2024年证明了GGM在一类"树构造"中的最优性，但能否仅用一次PRG调用构造PRF这一基本挑战仍然开放。本文旨在回答这一基本问题，即探索黑盒PRF构造中PRG调用次数的理论上限。

Method: 本文考虑全黑盒构造（构造和归约都是黑盒的），且归约与敌手的交互次数独立于敌手每次交互中调用其底层函数的次数。作者利用这种限制条件，通过归约结构和查询复杂度的分析，推导出非自适应调用的下界。具体地，证明了不存在o(n/log n)次非自适应调用PRG的构造，其中n是PRG的输入长度或in是PRF的输入长度。对输出足够的弱PRF，该下界在允许自适应查询时也成立。

Result: 主要结果是：在所述约束条件下，不存在需要o(n/log n)次非自适应PRG调用的PRF构造。这个不可能性对弱PRF（输出1比特）也成立，即使敌手只能做i.i.d.均匀随机查询。此外，对输出足够长的弱PRF，即使构造被允许自适应查询PRG，下界仍然成立。

Conclusion: 本文的结果将GGM构造的最优性从"树构造"这一受限类别推广到了更一般的全黑盒构造框架下（在特定归约交互约束下），为解决PRF从PRG的黑盒构造效率问题提供了重要进展。证明GGM构造成为了一个更广泛意义上的最优方案，尽管"仅一次PRG调用"的最终开放问题仍未完全解决。

Abstract: In their seminal work, Goldreich, Goldwasser, and Micali [CRYPTO 1984] constructed a pseudorandom function (PRF) using a black-box access to a pseudorandom generator (PRG). When combined with Levin's domain extension technique, the GGM construction invokes the PRG $ω(\log n)$ times, where $n$ denotes the input length to the PRG. To this day, no black-box construction achieving fewer calls is known.
  Recently, Beimel, Malkin, and Mazor [CRYPTO 2024] showed that for a certain family of constructions, which they termed \emph{tree constructions}, the GGM construction is optimal. However, the basic challenge of whether a PRF can be built with just \emph{one invocation} of the PRG still remains open.
  In this work, we consider fully black-box constructions of PRFs from PRGs, where both the construction and the reduction are required to be black-box, and the number of interactions the reduction makes with the adversary is independent of the number of oracle calls the adversary makes to its underlying function within each interaction. Our main result shows that no such construction can have $o(n/\log n)$ and $o(\mathsf{in}/\log\mathsf{in})$ \emph{non-adaptive} calls to the PRG, where $\mathsf{in}$ is the input length of the PRF. This impossibility holds even for weak PRFs with one-bit output, where the adversary is restricted to making i.i.d. uniformly random queries. In addition, we prove a lower bound for weak PRFs with sufficiently long outputs that holds even when the construction is allowed to make adaptive queries to the PRG.

</details>


### [178] [Trust Without Boundaries: An Architectural Analysis of Satellite Flight Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14532)
*Jack Vanlyssel,Gruia-Catalin Roman,Kendra Cook,Sazzadur Rahaman,Afsah Anwar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析NASA核心飞行软件(cFS)的安全架构，发现其将机载组件视为可信对等体，缺乏内部隔离和访问控制。通过在NOS3模拟器上实施五个恶意机载组件实验，验证了单一被攻破组件可利用广泛共享的权限实施难以与合法行为区分的攻击，并提出了强化内部信任边界的架构建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着航天器日益软件驱动和互联化，机载飞行软件已成为重要的安全边界。流行的飞行软件架构通常将机载组件视为可信对等体，简化了集成但限制了内部隔离和访问控制，需要研究这种安全隐患。

Method: 使用NASA的flight-representative的NOS3模拟器，分析cFS中权威、身份、通信、可观测性和持久性如何分布在机载组件之间。通过五个实验，用恶意机载组件滥用合法架构特权来验证弱点，并将cFS与其他模块化飞行软件框架进行比较以识别共性信任假设。

Result: 结果证明单个被攻破的组件可以利用广泛共享的权限，以难以与合法行为区分的方式发起攻击；同时发现cFS与其他模块化飞行软件框架存在重复出现的信任假设和架构弱点。

Conclusion: 提出架构级影响评估，并讨论在未来飞行软件系统中加强内部信任边界的机制。

Abstract: As spacecraft become more software-driven and interconnected, onboard flight software is an increasingly important security boundary. Popular flight software architectures often treat onboard components as trusted peers, simplifying integration while limiting internal isolation and access control. We analyze NASA's Core Flight Software (cFS) to examine how authority, identity, communication, observability, and persistence are distributed across onboard components. Using NASA's flight-representative NOS3 simulator, we validate these weaknesses through five experiments implemented with a malicious onboard component that abuses legitimate architectural privileges. We then compare cFS with other modular flight software frameworks to identify recurring trust assumptions and architectural weaknesses. Our results show that a single compromised component can exploit broadly shared authority in ways that are difficult to distinguish from legitimate behavior. We conclude with architectural implications and discuss mechanisms for strengthening internal trust boundaries in future flight software systems.

</details>


### [179] [Finding Vulnerabilities via LLM-Augmented Semantics-Aware Type-Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14533)
*Ruizhe Wang,Meng Xu,N. Asokan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SETYPE，一种基于语义感知的类型系统，利用大语言模型（LLM）直接从源代码中提取符号和表达式的自然语言语义，通过类型推断和类型检查来检测软件漏洞。作者实现了原型PYSETYPE用于检测Python Web应用漏洞，在实际应用中达到87%的检测精度和88%的检测准确率，并发现了15个潜在的零日漏洞，其中9个获得开发者确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于静态分析的漏洞检测依赖安全专家手工编码不安全模式的算法规则，这种方法过于关注语法模式而忽略了代码中更深的语义信息（如变量和函数名的含义）。随着软件系统日益复杂，仅用语法规则对漏洞进行建模变得愈发困难。

Method: 提出SETYPE——一种语义感知的类型系统，可直接从源代码中基于自然语言中符号和表达式的含义推导而来。在SETYPE系统中，类型推断和类型检查均由大语言模型（LLM）执行，类型检查失败即表示存在潜在漏洞。并实现了PYSETYPE原型来验证SETYPE在Python Web应用漏洞检测中的可行性。

Result: 在真实世界应用上的评估达到了87%的检测精度和88%的检测准确率。通过PYSETYPE识别出15个潜在的零日漏洞，其中9个已被开发者确认。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的语义感知类型系统能够有效利用代码中的自然语言语义信息来检测软件漏洞，弥补传统语法规则方法的不足，具有较高的检测准确性和实际发现新漏洞（零日漏洞）的能力。

Abstract: Vulnerability detection via static analysis traditionally relies on security experts encoding insecure coding patterns into algorithmic rules. However, this approach often focuses on syntactic patterns and overlooks deeper semantic information in the code, such as the meanings of variable and function names. As software systems grow more complex, modeling vulnerabilities using only syntactic rules becomes increasingly challenging.
  In this paper, we propose a semantics-aware approach to detecting software vulnerabilities. We present SETYPE, a semantics-aware type system that can be derived directly from source code based solely on the meanings of symbols and expressions in natural language. In the SETYPE type system, both type inference and checking are performed by Large Language Models (LLMs), and a failed type check indicates a potential vulnerability.
  We prototype PYSETYPE to demonstrate the feasibility of SETYPE for detecting vulnerabilities in Python web applications. Our evaluation on real-world applications achieves 87% detection precision and 88% detection accuracy. Using PYSETYPE, we identified 15 potential zero-day vulnerabilities, nine of which were confirmed by developers.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [180] [Learning Unsteady Aneurysm Hemodynamics with Physics-Informed DeepONets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13629)
*Oscar L. Cruz-Gonzalez,Valérie Deplano,Badih Ghattas*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种改进的多输入多输出物理信息深度算子网络（M3PI-DeepONet），用于预测腹主动脉瘤（AAA）理想化几何中的非定常血流，仅需0.3%标记内部数据即可同时预测3D速度和压力场，平均L2速度误差低于4%、压力误差约5%，推理速度比CFD快约36倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床上可操作的患者特异性血流动力学评估（壁面剪切应力、涡结构、压力分布）对判断AAA的危险演变至关重要。尽管PI-DeepONets在补充CFD方面表现良好，但面对复杂3D流动仍存在架构层面的挑战，需要更优的网络架构来提升预测精度与效率。

Method: 提出M3PI-DeepONet架构，核心是聚合注入（Aggregated Injection）策略：来自多个输入分支的潜在表征在trunk注入之前进行融合，使坐标基适应多个物理约束。这是首个将逐层门控机制与多分支算子网络拓扑相结合的架构，形成输入自适应trunk基。模型以3D Navier-Stokes方程为物理约束，基于物理信息残差、初始条件和边界条件进行训练，仅需0.3%标记内部数据及选定分支条件信号。

Result: M3PI-DeepONet同时预测非定常3D血流速度和压力场，平均相对L2速度误差低于4%，压力误差约5%；在分支输入条件可用后，相比于参考CFD仿真实现约36倍的保守保留周期推理加速。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了深度学习在心血管疾病建模中的应用，向实时、无创的临床诊断迈出了重要一步，验证了M3PI-DeepONet在AAA血流动力学预测中的有效性和高效性。

Abstract: Clinically actionable, patient-specific hemodynamic assessment, specifically wall shear stress, vortex structure and pressure distributions, is critical for determining risky or unfavorable evolution in Abdominal Aortic Aneurysms (AAA). While Physics-Informed Deep Operator Networks (PI-DeepONets) show promising results in complementing established 5 tools such as Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD), a persistent architectural challenge remains for complex 3D flows. In this direction, we propose a Modified Multi-Input Multi-Output PI-DeepONets (M3PI-DeepONet) designed for predicting unsteady flows in an idealized AAA geometry. Central to our model is the Aggregated Injection strategy, where latent representations from multiple input branches are fused prior to trunk injection, allowing the coordinate basis to adapt to multiple physical constraints. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first architecture to combine the layer-wise gating mechanism with a multi-branch operator-network topology, yielding an input-adaptive trunk basis. Additionally, we integrate the 3D Navier-Stokes equations as governing physical laws, so the model is trained based on physics-informed residuals, initial and boundary conditions, and only 0.3% of the labeled internal data together with the selected branch-conditioning signals. The M3PI-DeepONet simultaneously predicts unsteady 3D flow velocity and pressure fields with an average relative L2 velocity error below 4% and pressure error around 5% while achieving a conservative retained-cycle inference speedup of approximately 36x compared to reference CFD simulations once the branch inputs used for conditioning are available. This work advances the application of deep learning in cardiovascular disease modeling, marking step toward real-time, non-invasive clinical diagnostics.

</details>


### [181] [On the Brittleness of Maximum Likelihood Estimation for Gaussian Process Hyperparameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13793)
*Tyler R. Johnson,Kian Ben-Jacob,Christopher P. Muller,Ramin Bostanabad*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文评估了机器学习在工程设计中用于训练高斯过程（GP）模型时，极大似然估计（MLE）方法的脆弱性，提出了基于理论度量指标的替代方案，实验证明该方法在贝叶斯优化等下游任务中表现更优，甚至能超越表格基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLE是训练ML模型最流行的方法之一，但其假设条件不满足时会导致模型泛化能力差。高斯过程被广泛用于工程设计且常被错误地认为对过拟合非常鲁棒，但实际上MLE的脆弱性同样影响GP训练，因此需要系统性评估并提出解决方案。

Method: 作者从根本上评估了MLE在GP用于概率回归或分类任务时的脆弱性，将基于理论依据的度量指标与MLE进行对比，并提出实用的替代训练方案，用于构建更准确和鲁棒的高斯过程模型。

Result: 大量实验研究表明，所提出的解决方案在贝叶斯优化等下游设计任务中有效，构建的GP模型在预测精度、不确定性量化和推理成本方面甚至优于表格基础模型（如TabPFN）。

Conclusion: MLE在GP训练中确实存在脆弱性，基于理论度量的替代方案能为实践者提供构建准确且鲁棒GP的蓝图，相关贡献已公开发布在GitHub上。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) has become an indispensable part of modern engineering design workflows. A crucial step in training an ML model is the selection of the loss function which can be systematically formulated via various techniques such as maximum likelihood estimation (MLE) and cross-validation . While MLE is one of the most popular, effective, and intuitive mechanisms for training ML models, it is brittle: if the assumptions underpinning it are not met, the trained ML model may generalize poorly. This brittleness affects even Gaussian processes (GPs) which are widely used in engineering design and are often (incorrectly) presumed to be very robust to overfitting. In this paper, we fundamentally evaluate the brittleness of MLE in the context of training GPs for probabilistic regression or classification tasks. We compare theoretically grounded metrics against MLE and propose practical solutions. Our extensive studies demonstrate the effectiveness of our solutions in downstream design tasks such as Bayesian optimization and provide a blueprint for practitioners to build accurate and robust GPs that can even outperform tabular foundation models in terms of prediction accuracy, uncertainty quantification, and inference cost. Our contributions are publicly available via GitHub at https://github.com/Bostanabad-Research-Group/GP-vs-TabPFN-vs-GPyTorch.

</details>


### [182] [Deep Vision in Smart Manufacturing: MODERN Framework for Intelligent Quality Monitoring and Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13937)
*Yicheng Kang,Yuling Jiao,Xin Geng,Mahesh Nagarajan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出MODERN框架，一种基于深度学习的工业质量监控与故障隔离方法。采用inception残差神经网络架构构建控制图监控产品缺陷概率，利用迁移学习进行故障区域估计。针对训练数据不足的场景，提出小样本迁移监控技术和假设检验方法来评估方法适用性。理论上建立了缺陷似然估计和故障诊断的极小极大最优收敛率，实证表明在模拟和真实数据上均优于最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能制造过程中安装了大量传感器和成像设备，产生了丰富的数据资源，但现有工业质量控制方法未能充分利用这些增强能力。需要开发一种能整合这些能力的深度学习框架，实现更有效的质量监控和缺陷检测，同时需要考虑训练数据不足的现实约束。

Method: 1) 采用inception残差神经网络架构构建控制图来监控产品含缺陷的似然概率；2) 提出基于迁移学习的故障区域估计器来识别缺陷区域；3) 提出仅需小样本的迁移监控技术以应对训练数据不足的情况；4) 建立假设检验方法用于定量评估方法适用性；5) 理论上推导缺陷似然估计和故障诊断的极小极大最优收敛率。

Result: 理论和实证结果均优异：建立了极小极大最优收敛率；在模拟实验和真实数据上与最先进方法对比表现出显著优越性能；并得出了反直觉的管理启示——制造商并非总是应该不计成本地持续升级监控设备。

Conclusion: MODERN框架有效结合深度学习与质量控制，为智能制造提供了强有力的质量监控和故障隔离工具。其理论保证和实证表现共同支持其实际应用价值，同时揭示了设备升级决策中的经济考量，为管理者提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Smart manufacturing processes are often installed with a large number of sensors, imaging devices and computers, which not only enable instant communication across various modules of a production system but also aid in intelligent manufacturing management. In this paper, we introduce MODERN, a deep learning framework for quality monitoring and fault isolation, which integrates these enhanced capabilities into the practice of industrial quality control. Using the architecture of an inception residual neural network, we develop a control chart that monitors the likelihood of a product containing defects. We also propose a faulty region estimator that identifies the defective area using transfer learning. To extend our framework to cases where there are not sufficient training data, we suggest a transfer monitoring technique that requires only a small sample size and a hypothesis testing approach for quantitatively assessing the applicability of our method. Theoretically, we establish the minimax optimal convergence rate for both our defect likelihood estimation and fault diagnosis. Our results lead to a seemingly counter-intuitive managerial implication - it may not always be in a manufacturer's best interests to keep upgrading its monitoring equipment regardless of the cost. Empirically, we demonstrate the superior performance of our method in comparison with a state-of-the-art approach using both simulated experiments and real data.

</details>


### [183] [Extending Occam's inversion with lasso fusion, overcomplete dictionaries, and isotropic total variation regularisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14225)
*Anandaroop Ray*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统阐述了将l1正则化纳入Occam反演框架的理论与方法。作者展示了在一维情形下，lasso融合通过合成或分析路径均收敛于同一问题，并与统计学中的fused lasso对应；随后将框架扩展到多维及字典（如小波变换）。文中剖析了坐标下降、迭代加权最小二乘（IRLS）和分裂Bregman三种求解器的数学本质，并通过线性（1D回归、2D去模糊）和非线性（1D航空瞬变电磁）问题及野外数据验证。推荐1D问题使用坐标下降，2D问题使用IRLS（比分裂Bregman少约一个数量级的最小二乘求解次数）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Occam反演使用l2模型粗糙度约束，虽稳健但产生过于平滑的模型，限制了其分辨率；而l1正则化的地球物理反演长期与统计学和成像文献中更成熟的l1理论脱节。特别是在多维l1正则化（全变分）中存在两种形式但只有一种对边缘方向不变，这一区别在地球物理领域常被忽视。作者希望将l1正则化系统融入Occam框架，并厘清其数学基础，以促使地球物理学家更广泛地采用l1正则化反演。

Method: 作者在Occam框架内，针对l2数据范数与l1模型正则化组合，从理论上证明一维lasso融合的合成（synthesis）与分析（analysis）两种路径等价；将框架推广到多维和字典算子（如小波变换）。随后对三种常用l1求解器——坐标下降、IRLS和分裂Bregman——进行数学统一剖析，并在一系列从线性（1D回归、2D去模糊）到非线性（1D航空瞬变电磁含野外数据）问题上进行求解与对比。

Result: 理论证明表明一维fused lasso与Occam-l1框架一致；多维中强调了边缘方向不变性的关键区别。数值实验对比显示：1D问题中坐标下降效率最优；2D问题中IRLS表现优于分裂Bregman，所需最小二乘求解次数少约一个数量级。所有方法均成功应用于1D航空瞬变电磁反演及野外数据。

Conclusion: 本文为l1正则化地球物理反演提供了统一的Occam视角，澄清了合成/分析路径、多维全变分形式等长期被忽视的理论问题，并给出了面向不同问题的求解器实用推荐（1D用坐标下降，2D用IRLS）。这有助于地球物理界弥合与统计及成像文献之间的理论差距，促进l1正则化反演的更广泛采用。

Abstract: Occam's inversion is a robust algorithm to perform nonlinear geophysical inversion. It provides the smoothest model within observation noise, thereby discouraging geological overinterpretation. While Occam originally penalised l2 model roughness, l1 can be used to provide models that are visually sharp. However, l1 regularised geophysical inversion has diverged from the larger body of statistics and imaging literature. For example, l1 regularisation with a difference operator (i.e., total variation) in multiple dimensions has two distinct forms, only one of which is invariant to edge orientation -- a distinction often overlooked in geophysics. In one dimension this reduces to the fused lasso problem in statistics. Within the Occam framework, for l2 data norm and l1 model regularisation, we show that lasso fusion through either synthesis or analysis leads to the same 1D problem. We extend the framework to multiple dimensions and to operators such as wavelet transforms in a dictionary, unravelling the mathematics behind three commonly used solvers for l1 regularised problems. These are coordinate descent, iteratively reweighted least squares (IRLS), and split Bregman. We use them to solve a sequence of problems that are linear (1D regression, 2D deblurring) and nonlinear (1D airborne transient electromagnetics) including a field data example. We recommend coordinate descent for 1D problems, and IRLS over split Bregman for 2D problems, with IRLS requiring up to an order of magnitude fewer least squares solves. We hope this work will further encourage geophysicists to adopt l1 regularised inversion, by presenting its various forms as a familiar Occam's inversion.

</details>


### [184] [Offline Deep Q* Estimation with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14401)
*Xiaohong Chen,Yuling Jiao,Lican Kang,Jerry Zhijian Yang,Chen Zhong*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的离线强化学习框架，将最优贝尔曼算子估计与价值函数学习解耦：先用条件扩散模型估计奖励函数和转移核，再将估计器代入贝尔曼方程，通过最小化经验贝尔曼残差来学习深度估计器Q*。理论上建立了贝尔曼算子学习的非渐近收敛率、贝尔曼残差风险的oracle速率以及L2收敛速率，且不依赖深度RL常用的完备性假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线RL中估计最优动作价值函数Q*可归结为仅基于离线观测求解最优贝尔曼方程，但核心挑战在于奖励函数和转移核未知，导致最优贝尔曼算子无法直接从数据中观测，需要新的方法来解决这一算子不可观测的根本性问题。

Method: 提出解耦框架：第一阶段利用条件扩散模型估计奖励分布和转移核，由此构建数据驱动的贝尔曼算子逼近；第二阶段将估计器代入贝尔曼方程，在神经网络函数类上最小化经验贝尔曼残差以得到Q*的深度估计器。理论分析涵盖条件扩散估计的端到端全变差距离分析、贝尔曼残差风险的oracle速率以及L2收敛速率。

Result: 提出非渐近收敛理论成果：贝尔曼算子的估计收敛率；贝尔曼残差风险为O(n^{-2β/(d_x+d_a+2β)})；在可集中性条件下Q*的L2收敛率O(n^{-β/(d_x+d_a+2β)})，其中β为Q*的Hölder光滑度。理论不依赖完备性假设，实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过解耦算子估计与价值函数学习，成功绕过了离线RL中贝尔曼算子不可观测的瓶颈，在理论上给出了清晰的最优收敛速率，且摆脱了深层RL理论中常见的完备性假设限制，数值实验证实了其实用性能。

Abstract: In offline RL, estimating the optimal action-value function $Q^*$ can be formulated as solving the optimal Bellman equation based solely on offline observations. A fundamental challenge is that the reward function and transition kernel are unknown, so the optimal Bellman operator is not directly observable from data. To address this issue, we propose a novel framework that decouples operator estimation from value function learning. In this approach, we first formulate conditional diffusion models to estimate the reward law and transition kernel, which induces a data-driven approximation of the optimal Bellman operator. We then plug these estimators into the Bellman equation and obtain a deep estimator of $Q^*$ by minimizing the empirical Bellman residual over a neural network function class. Theoretically, we first establish sharp nonasymptotic convergence rates for learning the optimal Bellman operator through an end-to-end analysis of conditional diffusion estimation in total variation distance. We then establish the oracle value-stage rate $\widetilde{\mathcal O}\bigl(n^{-\frac{2β}{d_x+d_a+2β}}\bigr)$ for the excess Bellman residual risk. Finally, under a concentrability condition, we translate this residual bound into an $L^2$ convergence rate of $\widetilde{\mathcal O}\bigl(n^{-\fracβ{d_x+d_a+2β}}\bigr)$ for the resulting deep estimator of $Q^*$, where $d_x$ and $d_a$ denote the dimensions of the state and action spaces, respectively, and $β$ denotes the Hölder smoothness index of $Q^*$. Importantly, our theoretical analysis does not rely on completeness assumptions commonly used in deep RL theory. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method and its strong empirical performance.

</details>


### [185] [Online Inference in Distributional Temporal-Difference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14408)
*Yang Peng,Liangyu Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了固定策略下回报分布函数泛函的在线统计推断问题。作者采用非参数分布时序差分学习从单条马尔可夫轨迹估计回报分布，证明了Polyak-Ruppert平均估计器的根号T误差在Cramér空间中弱收敛于中心化高斯随机元，并证明了基于观测轨迹的条件自助法收敛到相同的高斯极限。对于光滑统计泛函（如方差、CVaR、期望损失和期望分位数），该结果支持自助法推断；对于非光滑统计泛函（如回报分位数），作者建立了局部渐近理论及其自助法对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中回报分布的统计推断是重要但具有挑战性的问题。现有工作大多关注回报分布的估计一致性，但缺乏对估计误差的分布信息，因此难以对回报分布的光滑或不光滑泛函进行置信区间构建和假设检验。本文旨在为固定策略下的回报分布函数泛函提供严格的在线统计推断理论基础。

Method: 采用非参数分布时序差分学习（distributional temporal-difference learning）从单条马尔可夫轨迹估计回报分布。对Polyak-Ruppert平均估计器，在Cramér空间（即带加权范数的分布函数空间）中建立根号T误差的中心极限定理；同时建立条件自助法的弱收敛理论。对于光滑统计泛函，利用泛函delta方法进行推断；对于非光滑统计泛函（由CDF方程刻画，如分位数），在T^{-1/2}邻域内建立局部渐近理论及其自助法对应。

Result: 证明了：(1) Polyak-Ruppert平均估计器的根号T误差弱收敛到Cramér空间中的中心化高斯随机元；(2) 条件于观测轨迹的自助法根号T差分布弱收敛到同一高斯极限。这些结果为方差、CVaR、期望损失和期望分位数等光滑统计泛函的自助法推断提供了理论保障，并为回报分位数等非光滑泛函建立了局部渐近推断理论。

Conclusion: 本文为强化学习中固定策略回报分布泛函的在线推断提供了完整的理论框架，涵盖了光滑和非光滑统计泛函两大类情形，并通过自助法实现了可操作的推断程序。该工作为强化学习中的不确定性量化提供了坚实的统计理论基础。

Abstract: We study online statistical inference for functionals of the return distribution under a fixed policy. The return distribution is estimated by nonparametric distributional temporal-difference learning from a single Markov trajectory. For the Polyak--Ruppert averaged estimator, we prove that its root-$T$ error converges weakly to a centered Gaussian random element in Cramér space. We also prove that, conditionally on the observed trajectory, the root-$T$ difference between the bootstrap and original averages converges weakly to the same Gaussian limit. These results justify bootstrap inference for smooth statistical functionals, including variance, CVaR, expected shortfall, and expectiles. For nonsmooth statistical functionals, we develop a local asymptotic theory for the estimated return CDF over $T^{-1/2}$-neighborhoods of finitely many thresholds, together with its bootstrap analogue. This theory allows us to conduct inference for nonsmooth statistical functionals characterized by CDF equations, including return quantiles.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [Generalization Error Estimation for Primal--Dual Algorithms in Non-Smooth Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13870)
*Kai Tan,Pierre C Bellec*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对非光滑回归中原对偶算法的优化轨迹，提出了一种轨迹级的泛化误差估计框架。通过使用过去对偶迭代的加权组合来修正样本内拟合值，构建了无协方差、数据驱动的风险估计器，并在高维高斯设计下给出了有限样本保证，同时在多方根岭回归中建立了超越高斯设计的匹配高斯普适性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于ℓ1惩罚最小绝对偏差回归和平方根Lasso等非光滑回归问题，数据拟合损失不可微，现有的针对梯度型优化路径的风险估计器无法直接适用。因此需要为原对偶算法路径开发专用的泛化误差估计方法。

Method: 提出一个包含Chambolle-Pock算法及相关原对偶分裂方法的通用递归框架。核心思想是用过去对偶迭代值的加权组合来修正每个样本内拟合值。理想权重为Stein导数收缩量且依赖于设计协方差；文章从可观测的拟合信号轨迹的导数收缩量构造替代权重，得到无协方差、数据驱动的修正。对于高维高斯设计和固定有限迭代次数，证明了两类估计器的有限样本保证。

Result: 在高维高斯设计下，对有限迭代次数情形证明了所提出两类估计器的有限样本保证；在平方根岭回归中建立了超越高斯设计的匹配高斯普适性结果。数值实验表明所提估计器能够准确追踪有限优化路径上的样本外风险。

Conclusion: 本文成功地将轨迹级泛化误差估计从光滑梯度型优化路径推广到非光滑的原对偶优化路径，所提出的协方差无关、数据驱动的修正方法在理论上具有有限样本保证，在实践中能准确追踪样本外风险。

Abstract: This paper studies trajectory-wise estimation of generalization error for primal--dual algorithms in non-smooth regression. Motivating examples include \(\ell_1\)-penalized least absolute deviations regression and square-root Lasso regression, where the data-fitting loss is non-differentiable and existing risk estimators for gradient-type optimization paths do not apply directly. We develop a general recursive framework that includes the Chambolle--Pock algorithm and related primal--dual splitting methods. We estimate risk by correcting each in-sample fitted value with a weighted combination of past dual iterates. The ideal weights are Stein derivative contractions and depend on the design covariance. We construct replacement weights from observable derivative contractions of the fitted-signal trajectory, yielding a covariance-free, data-driven correction. For high-dimensional Gaussian designs and fixed finite iteration horizon, we prove finite-sample guarantees for both estimators. For square-root ridge, we further establish a matched-Gaussian universality result beyond Gaussian designs. Numerical experiments show that the proposed estimators accurately track the out-of-sample risk along finite optimization paths.

</details>


### [187] [Sharp Minimax Theory for Randomized Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13822)
*Timothy Sudijono,Edgar Dobriban,Eric Tchetgen Tchetgen*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究有限总体随机实验中样本平均处理效应（SATE）的极小极大最优设计与估计器。对于二元潜在结果，作者将极小极大风险等价转化为仅含2个未知参数的估计问题，并建立了二阶风险展开式，证明该风险由伯努利随机化搭配非线性收缩估计量达到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准程序（如完全随机化搭配差分均值估计）仅在一阶意义上极小极大最优，其高阶性能可能并非最优。作者希望建立有限总体随机实验中小样本乃至高阶意义上的极小极大理论，填补一阶最优性之外的空白。

Method: 通过将二元潜在结果下的极小极大风险约化为含2个未知参数的估计问题，利用Airy函数推导显式常数C，建立二阶风险展开式。同时构造伯努利随机化与非线性收缩估计量以实现该极小极大风险，并分析相关程序的可容许性。

Result: 极小极大风险满足 ρ_n^* = n^{-1} - Cn^{-4/3} + o(n^{-4/3})，其中C为与Airy函数相关的显式常数。该风险由伯努利随机化配合非线性收缩估计量达到。标准程序（完全随机化+差分均值）仅在n的一阶意义上极小极大最优。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了在有限总体随机实验中，高阶极小极大性质需要更精细的随机化方案与估计器设计；标准程序虽然一阶最优，但在高阶意义上并非最优，这对实际实验设计具有指导意义。

Abstract: We study minimax-optimal designs and estimators for estimating the sample average treatment effect in finite population randomized experiments, where both design and estimator are unrestricted. For binary potential outcomes, we show this minimax risk is equivalent to the minimax risk $ρ_n^*$ of an estimation problem with $2$ unknown parameters. We leverage this reduction to establish a second-order risk expansion $ρ_n^* = n^{-1} - Cn^{-4/3} + o_n(n^{-4/3})$ for an explicit constant $C$ related to the Airy function. The minimax risk is attained by Bernoulli randomization with a nonlinear shrinkage estimator. Our results show that standard procedures such as complete randomization with difference in means are only minimax optimal up to first order in $n.$ We derive further results on admissibility of these procedures and discuss the practical implications of our results.

</details>


### [188] [Converse bounds for multiple graph alignment and correlation detection based on last matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14450)
*Taha Ameen,Bruce Hajek*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种信息论下界推导方法，用于m个相关图的对齐与相关性检测问题。核心思路是：当m≥3时，若前m-1个图的对齐结果由预言机（genie）额外揭示，问题就简化为最后一个图与其余图的匹配问题（即"最后匹配"问题），该问题等价于m=2时的两图对齐问题，从而可将m=2的converse bound推广到更大的m。该方法不仅适用于对齐，还可用于弱相关性检测的converse bound。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于m个相关图的对齐和相关性检测，现有的converse bound主要针对m=2的情况，缺乏将下界扩展到更大m的通用方法。作者希望找到一种简单的思路，利用已揭示的部分对齐信息来简化问题结构，从而将两图情形的结论推广到多图情形。

Method: 核心方法是"预言机（genie）"思想：对于m≥3，假设已知m-1个图之间的对齐关系（作为额外信息），则剩下的核心难题就变为将第m个图与已对齐的m-1个图进行最后匹配。对于高斯模型和Erdős–Rényi模型，该最后匹配问题在信息论上等价于仅有两个观测图的情形，因此可以直接借用m=2的converse bound推导路径。

Result: 该方法成功地将m=2的converse bounds扩展到了任意m≥3的情形，适用于高斯模型和Erdős–Rényi模型。此外，作者还展示了这一方法同样可以扩展到弱相关性检测的converse bound推导，尽管这一应用比对齐情形更为微妙。

Conclusion: 通过"预言机揭示部分对齐"这一简单的信息论技巧，可以为多图对齐和相关性检测问题系统地建立converse bounds。该思路的关键在于将多图问题递归地降维为两图问题，并且该技巧对于对齐问题直观明显，对于弱检测问题也依然有效。

Abstract: The paper focuses on information theoretic converse bounds for the alignment of $m$ correlated graphs and for the detection of correlation among $m$ graphs. A simple idea for $m\geq 3$ is that if the alignment of $m-1$ of the graphs is revealed as extra information (by a genie for example) then it is still necessary to produce the alignment between the one remaining graph and the others, i.e. the last matching must be accomplished. For both Gaussian and Erdos-Renyi models, the last-matching problem is equivalent to one with two observed graphs, providing a path to extend converse bounds for $m=2$ to larger $m$. While the method is rather obvious for alignment, we show that the method can also be used to derive converse bounds for weak detection of correlation.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [L-FNO: Lorentzian Fourier Neural Operator for Stochastic Event Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13562)
*Songhee Kang,Jihoon Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Lorentzian傅里叶神经算子（L-FNO），一种将FNO风格协变量路径、Lorentzian谱核和基于似然的训练目标相结合的随机神经算子，用于解决稀疏事件场景中的条件强度估计问题，在八个合成点过程基准和三个真实数据集上优于回归式和似然式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代运筹系统即使在常规条件下也面临不确定性，罕见、突发且自激的事件同时源自外生协变量和内生事件动态。标准神经算子通常训练为回归式函数到函数模型而非条件强度估计器，限制了其在稀疏事件场景中的适用性。

Method: L-FNO是一种随机神经算子，包含三个关键组件：(1) FNO风格的协变量路径用于处理外生输入；(2) Lorentzian谱核用于建模历史依赖的激励效应（自激机制）；(3) 基于似然的训练目标来优化模型参数。

Result: 在八个合成点过程基准和三个真实数据集（疾病暴发预测、半导体故障/缺陷检测）上进行评估，L-FNO在事件似然、校准诊断和罕见事件检测方面均优于回归式和似然式神经算子基线。

Conclusion: 结果表明，结构化谱记忆和基于似然的学习为神经算子模型建模随机事件动态提供了有效的归纳偏置。

Abstract: Modern operational systems face uncertainty even in routine conditions, where rare, bursty, and self-exciting events emerge from both exogenous covariates and endogenous event dynamics.
